1→"""Context building for review: ReviewContext, shared helpers, heuristic signals."""

     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.discovery.api import (
    10→    disable_file_cache,
    11→    enable_file_cache,
    12→    is_file_cache_enabled,
    13→    read_file_text,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.core.pathing.api import rel, resolve_path
    16→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import ReviewContext
    17→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.patterns import (
    18→    CLASS_NAME_RE,
    19→    ERROR_PATTERNS,
    20→    FUNC_NAME_RE,
    21→    NAME_PREFIX_RE,
    22→    default_review_module_patterns,

    30→# ── Shared helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    31→
    32→
    33→def abs_path(filepath: str) -> str:
    34→    """Resolve filepath to absolute using resolve_path."""
    35→    return resolve_path(filepath)
    36→
    37→
    38→def file_excerpt(filepath: str, max_lines: int = 30) -> str | None:
    39→    """Read first *max_lines* of a file, returning the text or None."""
    40→    content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
    41→    if content is None:
    42→        return None
    43→    lines = content.splitlines(keepends=True)
    44→    if len(lines) <= max_lines:
    45→        return content
    46→    return "".join(lines[:max_lines]) + f"\n... ({len(lines) - max_lines} more lines)"
    47→
    48→
    49→def dep_graph_lookup(
    50→    graph: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
    51→    filepath: str,
    52→) -> dict[str, object]:
    53→    """Look up a file in the dep graph, trying absolute and relative keys."""
    54→    resolved = resolve_path(filepath)
    55→    entry = graph.get(resolved)
    56→    if entry is not None:
    57→        return entry
    58→    # Try relative path
    59→    rpath = rel(filepath)
    60→    entry = graph.get(rpath)
    61→    if entry is not None:
    62→        return entry
    63→    return {}
    64→
    65→
    66→def importer_count(entry: dict) -> int:
    67→    """Extract importer count from a dep graph entry."""
    68→    importers = entry.get("importers", set())
    69→    if isinstance(importers, set):
    70→        return len(importers)
    71→    return entry.get("importer_count", 0)
    72→
    73→
    74→# ── Per-file review context builder ──────────────────────────────
    75→
    76→
    77→def build_review_context(
    78→    path: Path,
    79→    lang,
    80→    state: dict,
    81→    files: list[str] | None = None,
    82→) -> ReviewContext:
    83→    """Gather codebase conventions for contextual evaluation.
    84→
    85→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks).
    86→    """
    87→    if files is None:
    88→        files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
    89→    ctx = ReviewContext()
    90→
    91→    if not files:
    92→        return ctx
    93→
    94→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
    95→    if not already_cached:
    96→        enable_file_cache()
    97→    try:
    98→        return _build_review_context_inner(files, lang, state, ctx)
    99→    finally:
   100→        if not already_cached:
   101→            disable_file_cache()
   102→
   103→
   104→def _collect_file_contents(files: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
   105→    """Read all review files once (with discovery cache enabled)."""
   106→    file_contents: dict[str, str] = {}
   107→    for filepath in files:
   108→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   109→        if content is not None:
   110→            file_contents[filepath] = content
   111→    return file_contents
   112→
   113→
   114→def _build_naming_vocabulary(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, object]:
   115→    """Extract naming prefix distribution from function/class names."""
   116→    prefix_counter: Counter = Counter()
   117→    total_names = 0
   118→    for content in file_contents.values():
   119→        for name in FUNC_NAME_RE.findall(content) + CLASS_NAME_RE.findall(content):
   120→            total_names += 1
   121→            match = NAME_PREFIX_RE.match(name)
   122→            if match:
   123→                prefix_counter[match.group(1)] += 1
   124→    return {
   125→        "prefixes": dict(prefix_counter.most_common(20)),
   126→        "total_names": total_names,
   127→    }
   128→
   129→
   130→def _build_error_conventions(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, int]:
   131→    """Summarize per-file error-handling pattern presence."""
   132→    error_counts: Counter = Counter()
   133→    for content in file_contents.values():
   134→        for pattern_name, pattern in ERROR_PATTERNS.items():
   135→            if pattern.search(content):
   136→                error_counts[pattern_name] += 1
   137→    return dict(error_counts)
   138→
   139→
   140→def _module_patterns_function(lang):
   141→    """Resolve module-pattern hook with fallback default implementation."""
   142→    module_pattern_fn = getattr(lang, "review_module_patterns_fn", None)
   143→    if callable(module_pattern_fn):
   144→        return module_pattern_fn
   145→    return default_review_module_patterns
   146→
   147→
   148→def _build_module_patterns(file_contents: dict[str, str], lang) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
   149→    """Build per-directory module pattern prevalence summary."""
   150→    dir_patterns: dict[str, Counter] = {}
   151→    module_pattern_fn = _module_patterns_function(lang)
   152→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
   153→        parts = Path(filepath).parts
   154→        if len(parts) < 2:
   155→            continue
   156→        dir_name = parts[-2] + "/"
   157→        counter = dir_patterns.setdefault(dir_name, Counter())
   158→        pattern_names = module_pattern_fn(content)
   159→        if not isinstance(pattern_names, list | tuple | set):
   160→            pattern_names = default_review_module_patterns(content)
   161→        for pattern_name in pattern_names:
   162→            counter[pattern_name] += 1
   163→        if re.search(r"\bclass\s+\w+", content):
   164→            counter["class_based"] += 1
   165→    return {
   166→        directory: dict(counter.most_common(3))
   167→        for directory, counter in dir_patterns.items()
   168→        if sum(counter.values()) >= 3
   169→    }
   170→
   171→
   172→def _build_import_graph_summary(lang) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
   173→    """Build top imported file summary from dep graph."""
   174→    if not lang.dep_graph:
   175→        return {}
   176→    importer_counts = {}
   177→    for filepath, entry in lang.dep_graph.items():
   178→        count = importer_count(entry)
   179→        if count > 0:
   180→            importer_counts[rel(filepath)] = count
   181→    top = sorted(importer_counts.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1])[:20]
   182→    return {"top_imported": dict(top)}
   183→
   184→
   185→def _allowed_review_files(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:
   186→    """Return normalized active review file set."""
   187→    return {
   188→        rel(filepath)
   189→        for filepath in file_contents
   190→        if isinstance(filepath, str) and filepath
   191→    }
   192→
   193→
   194→def _build_existing_findings(
   195→    state: dict, *, allowed_review_files: set[str]
   196→) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   197→    """Build open-finding summaries scoped to active review files."""
   198→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   199→    by_file: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   200→    for finding in findings.values():
   201→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   202→            continue
   203→        finding_file_raw = finding.get("file", "")
   204→        if not isinstance(finding_file_raw, str) or not finding_file_raw:
   205→            continue
   206→        finding_file = rel(finding_file_raw)
   207→        if finding_file not in allowed_review_files:
   208→            continue
   209→        by_file.setdefault(finding_file, []).append(
   210→            f"{finding['detector']}: {finding['summary'][:80]}"
   211→        )
   212→    return by_file
   213→
   214→
   215→def _build_codebase_stats(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, int]:
   216→    """Compute codebase-level file/LOC summary stats."""
   217→    total_files = len(file_contents)
   218→    total_loc = sum(len(content.splitlines()) for content in file_contents.values())
   219→    return {
   220→        "total_files": total_files,
   221→        "total_loc": total_loc,
   222→        "avg_file_loc": total_loc // total_files if total_files else 0,
   223→    }
   224→
   225→
   226→def _build_sibling_conventions(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
   227→    """Summarize sibling naming-prefix conventions by directory."""
   228→    dir_functions: dict[str, Counter] = {}
   229→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
   230→        parts = Path(filepath).parts
   231→        if len(parts) < 2:
   232→            continue
   233→        dir_name = parts[-2] + "/"
   234→        counter = dir_functions.setdefault(dir_name, Counter())
   235→        for name in FUNC_NAME_RE.findall(content):
   236→            match = NAME_PREFIX_RE.match(name)
   237→            if match:
   238→                counter[match.group(1)] += 1
   239→    return {
   240→        directory: dict(counter.most_common(5))
   241→        for directory, counter in dir_functions.items()
   242→        if sum(counter.values()) >= 3
   243→    }
   244→
   245→
   246→def _build_error_strategies(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
   247→    """Classify dominant error strategy per file."""
   248→    strategies: dict[str, str] = {}
   249→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
   250→        strategy = classify_error_strategy(content)
   251→        if strategy:
   252→            strategies[rel(filepath)] = strategy
   253→    return strategies
   254→
   255→
   256→def _build_review_context_inner(
   257→    files: list[str],
   258→    lang,
   259→    state: dict,
   260→    ctx: ReviewContext,
   261→) -> ReviewContext:
   262→    """Inner context builder (runs with file cache enabled)."""
   263→    file_contents = _collect_file_contents(files)
   264→    ctx.naming_vocabulary = _build_naming_vocabulary(file_contents)
   265→    ctx.error_conventions = _build_error_conventions(file_contents)
   266→    ctx.module_patterns = _build_module_patterns(file_contents, lang)
   267→
   268→    import_graph_summary = _build_import_graph_summary(lang)
   269→    if import_graph_summary:
   270→        ctx.import_graph_summary = import_graph_summary
   271→
   272→    # 5. Zone distribution
   273→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   274→        ctx.zone_distribution = lang.zone_map.counts()
   275→
   276→    # 6. Existing findings per file (summaries only), scoped to active review files.
   277→    ctx.existing_findings = _build_existing_findings(
   278→        state, allowed_review_files=_allowed_review_files(file_contents)
   279→    )
   280→
   281→    # 7. Codebase stats
   282→    ctx.codebase_stats = _build_codebase_stats(file_contents)
   283→    _ = (
   284→        ctx.codebase_stats["total_files"],
   285→        ctx.codebase_stats["total_loc"],
   286→        ctx.codebase_stats["avg_file_loc"],
   287→    )
   288→
   289→    # 8. Sibling function conventions — what naming/patterns neighbors in same dir use
   290→    ctx.sibling_conventions = _build_sibling_conventions(file_contents)
   291→
   292→    # 9. AI debt signals
   293→    ctx.ai_debt_signals = gather_ai_debt_signals(file_contents, rel_fn=rel)
   294→
   295→    # 10. Auth patterns
   296→    ctx.auth_patterns = gather_auth_context(file_contents, rel_fn=rel)
   297→
   298→    # 11. Error strategies per file
   299→    ctx.error_strategies = _build_error_strategies(file_contents)
   300→
   301→    ctx.normalize_sections(strict=True)
   302→    return ctx
   303→
   304→
   305→def serialize_context(ctx: ReviewContext) -> dict[str, object]:
   306→    """Convert ReviewContext to a JSON-serializable dict."""
   307→    def _section_dict(value: object) -> dict:
   308→        if hasattr(value, "to_dict") and callable(value.to_dict):
   309→            data = value.to_dict()
   310→            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
   311→        return dict(value) if isinstance(value, dict) else {}
   312→
   313→    metrics = ("total_files", "total_loc", "avg_file_loc")
   314→    codebase_stats = _section_dict(ctx.codebase_stats)
   315→    out = {
   316→        "naming_vocabulary": _section_dict(ctx.naming_vocabulary),
   317→        "error_conventions": _section_dict(ctx.error_conventions),
   318→        "module_patterns": _section_dict(ctx.module_patterns),
   319→        "import_graph_summary": _section_dict(ctx.import_graph_summary),
   320→        "zone_distribution": _section_dict(ctx.zone_distribution),
   321→        "existing_findings": _section_dict(ctx.existing_findings),
   322→        "codebase_stats": {
   323→            key: int(codebase_stats.get(key, 0))
   324→            for key in metrics
   325→        },
   326→        "sibling_conventions": _section_dict(ctx.sibling_conventions),
   327→    }
   328→    if ctx.ai_debt_signals:
   329→        out["ai_debt_signals"] = _section_dict(ctx.ai_debt_signals)
   330→    if ctx.auth_patterns:
   331→        out["auth_patterns"] = _section_dict(ctx.auth_patterns)
   332→    if ctx.error_strategies:
   333→        out["error_strategies"] = _section_dict(ctx.error_strategies)
   334→    return out
   335→

    85→def _rel_list(s) -> list[str]:
    86→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    87→    if isinstance(s, set):
    88→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    89→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    90→
    91→
    92→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    93→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    94→    if value in (None, ""):
    95→        return None
    96→    parsed = int(value)
    97→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
    98→
    99→
   100→def _relative_to_base(filepath: str, *, resolved_base: Path) -> str | None:
   101→    """Return ``filepath`` relative to ``resolved_base`` when inside the base tree."""
   102→    try:
   103→        resolved = Path(filepath).resolve()
   104→        return resolved.relative_to(resolved_base).as_posix()
   105→    except ValueError:
   106→        return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def _collect_allowed_review_files(
   110→    files: list[str],
   111→    lang: object,
   112→    *,
   113→    base_path: Path | None = None,
   114→) -> set[str]:
   115→    """Return relative production-file paths allowed for holistic review batches."""
   116→    allowed: set[str] = set()
   117→    zone_map = getattr(lang, "zone_map", None)
   118→    resolved_base = base_path.resolve() if isinstance(base_path, Path) else None
   119→    for filepath in files:
   120→        if not isinstance(filepath, str):
   121→            continue
   122→        normalized = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/")
   123→        if not normalized:
   124→            continue
   125→        if zone_map is not None:
   126→            try:
   127→                zone = zone_map.get(filepath)
   128→                zone_value = getattr(zone, "value", str(zone))
   129→            except (AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
   130→                zone_value = "production"
   131→            if zone_value in _NON_PRODUCTION_ZONES:
   132→                continue
   133→        allowed.add(normalized)
   134→        allowed.add(rel(filepath))
   135→        if resolved_base is not None:
   136→            relative = _relative_to_base(filepath, resolved_base=resolved_base)
   137→            if relative is not None:
   138→                allowed.add(relative)
   139→    return allowed
   140→
   141→
   142→def _file_in_allowed_scope(filepath: object, allowed_files: set[str]) -> bool:
   143→    """True when *filepath* resolves to a currently in-scope review file."""
   144→    if not isinstance(filepath, str):
   145→        return False
   146→    normalized = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/")
   147→    if not normalized:
   148→        return False
   149→    if normalized in allowed_files:
   150→        return True
   151→    return rel(filepath) in allowed_files
   152→
   153→
   154→def _filter_issue_focus_to_scope(
   155→    issue_focus: object,
   156→    allowed_files: set[str],
   157→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   158→    """Drop out-of-scope related_files from historical issue focus payload."""
   159→    if not isinstance(issue_focus, dict):
   160→        return None
   161→    issues_raw = issue_focus.get("issues", [])
   162→    issues: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   163→    if isinstance(issues_raw, list):
   164→        for raw_issue in issues_raw:
   165→            if not isinstance(raw_issue, dict):
   166→                continue
   167→            issue = dict(raw_issue)
   168→            related_raw = issue.get("related_files", [])
   169→            if isinstance(related_raw, list):
   170→                issue["related_files"] = [
   171→                    path
   172→                    for path in related_raw
   173→                    if _file_in_allowed_scope(path, allowed_files)
   174→                ]
   175→            issues.append(issue)
   176→    scoped = dict(issue_focus)
   177→    scoped["issues"] = issues
   178→    scoped["selected_count"] = len(issues)
   179→    return scoped
   180→
   181→
   182→def _filter_batches_to_file_scope(
   183→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   184→    *,
   185→    allowed_files: set[str],
   186→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Strip out-of-scope files/signals from review batches."""
   188→    if not allowed_files:
   189→        return []
   190→
   191→    scoped_batches: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   192→    for raw_batch in batches:
   193→        if not isinstance(raw_batch, dict):
   194→            continue
   195→        batch = dict(raw_batch)
   196→        files_to_read = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   197→        normalized_files_to_read: list[Any] = []
   198→        if isinstance(files_to_read, list):
   199→            normalized_files_to_read = [
   200→                filepath
   201→                for filepath in files_to_read
   202→                if _file_in_allowed_scope(filepath, allowed_files)
   203→            ]
   204→        batch["files_to_read"] = normalized_files_to_read
   205→
   206→        concern_signals = batch.get("concern_signals", [])
   207→        if isinstance(concern_signals, list):
   208→            batch["concern_signals"] = [
   209→                signal
   210→                for signal in concern_signals
   211→                if isinstance(signal, dict)
   212→                and _file_in_allowed_scope(signal.get("file", ""), allowed_files)
   213→            ]
   214→            if "concern_signal_count" in batch:
   215→                batch["concern_signal_count"] = len(batch["concern_signals"])
   216→
   217→        issue_focus = _filter_issue_focus_to_scope(
   218→            batch.get("historical_issue_focus"),
   219→            allowed_files,
   220→        )
   221→        if issue_focus is not None:
   222→            batch["historical_issue_focus"] = issue_focus
   223→
   224→        has_seed_files = bool(batch["files_to_read"])
   225→        has_signals = bool(batch.get("concern_signals"))
   226→        if has_seed_files or has_signals:
   227→            scoped_batches.append(batch)
   228→    return scoped_batches
   229→
   230→
   231→def prepare_review(
   232→    path: Path,
   233→    lang: object,
   234→    state: dict,
   235→    options: ReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   236→) -> dict[str, object]:
   237→    """Prepare review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict.
   238→
   239→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks
   240→    when the caller already has the file list, e.g. from _setup_lang).
   241→    """
   242→    resolved_options = options or ReviewPrepareOptions()
   243→    resolved_options.max_files = _normalize_max_files(resolved_options.max_files)
   244→    all_files = (
   245→        resolved_options.files
   246→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   247→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   251→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.
   252→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   253→    if not already_cached:
   254→        enable_file_cache()
   255→    try:
   256→        context = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   257→        selected = select_files_for_review(
   258→            lang,
   259→            path,
   260→            state,
   261→            options=ReviewSelectionOptions(
   262→                max_files=resolved_options.max_files,
   263→                max_age_days=resolved_options.max_age_days,
   264→                force_refresh=resolved_options.force_refresh,
   265→                files=all_files,
   266→            ),
   267→        )
   268→        file_requests = _build_file_requests(selected, lang, state)
   269→    finally:
   270→        if not already_cached:
   271→            disable_file_cache()
   272→
   273→    default_dims, dimension_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   274→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   275→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   276→        config_dimensions=resolved_options.config_dimensions,
   277→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   278→    )
   279→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   280→    valid_dims = set(dimension_prompts)
   281→    invalid_requested = [
   282→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   283→    ]
   284→    invalid_config = [
   285→        dim
   286→        for dim in (resolved_options.config_dimensions or [])
   287→        if dim not in valid_dims
   288→    ]
   289→
   290→    return {
   291→        "command": "review",
   292→        "language": lang.name,
   293→        "dimensions": dims,
   294→        "dimension_prompts": {
   295→            d: dimension_prompts[d] for d in dims if d in dimension_prompts
   296→        },
   297→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   298→        "context": serialize_context(context),
   299→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   300→        "files": file_requests,
   301→        "total_candidates": len(file_requests),
   302→        "cache_status": {
   303→            "fresh": count_fresh(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   304→            "stale": count_stale(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   305→            "new": len(file_requests),
   306→        },
   307→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   308→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   309→            "config": invalid_config,
   310→        },
   311→    }
   312→
   313→
   314→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   315→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   316→    file_requests = []
   317→    for filepath in files:
   318→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   319→        if content is None:
   320→            continue
   321→
   322→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   323→        zone = "production"
   324→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
   325→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath).value
   326→
   327→        neighbors: dict
   328→        if lang.dep_graph:
   329→            entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   330→            imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
   331→            importers_raw = entry.get("importers", set())
   332→            importer_count_value = importer_count(entry)
   333→            neighbors = {
   334→                "imports": _rel_list(imports_raw),
   335→                "importers": _rel_list(importers_raw),
   336→                "importer_count": importer_count_value,
   337→            }
   338→        else:
   339→            neighbors = {}
   340→
   341→        file_requests.append(
   342→            {
   343→                "file": rpath,
   344→                "content": content,
   345→                "zone": zone,
   346→                "loc": len(content.splitlines()),
   347→                "neighbors": neighbors,
   348→                "existing_findings": get_file_findings(state, filepath),
   349→            }
   350→        )
   351→    return file_requests
   352→
   353→
   354→def prepare_holistic_review(
   355→    path: Path,
   356→    lang: object,
   357→    state: dict,
   358→    options: HolisticReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   359→) -> dict[str, object]:
   360→    """Prepare holistic review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict."""
   361→    resolved_options = options or HolisticReviewPrepareOptions()
   362→    all_files = (
   363→        resolved_options.files
   364→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   365→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   366→    )
   367→    allowed_review_files = _collect_allowed_review_files(
   368→        all_files,
   369→        lang,
   370→        base_path=path,
   371→    )
   372→
   373→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   374→    if not already_cached:
   375→        enable_file_cache()
   376→    try:
   377→        context = HolisticContext.from_raw(
   378→            build_holistic_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   379→        )
   380→        # Also include per-file review context for reference
   381→        review_ctx = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   382→    finally:
   383→        if not already_cached:
   384→            disable_file_cache()
   385→
   386→    default_dims, holistic_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   387→    _, per_file_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   388→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   389→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   390→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   391→    )
   392→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   393→    valid_dims = set(holistic_prompts) | set(per_file_prompts)
   394→    invalid_requested = [
   395→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   396→    ]
   397→    invalid_default = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim not in valid_dims]
   398→    batches = _build_investigation_batches(
   399→        context,
   400→        lang,
   401→        repo_root=path,
   402→        max_files_per_batch=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   403→    )
   404→
   405→    # Append design-coherence batch from mechanical concern signals.
   406→    concerns_batch = None
   407→    try:
   408→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang.name)
   409→        concerns = [
   410→            concern
   411→            for concern in concerns
   412→            if _file_in_allowed_scope(getattr(concern, "file", ""), allowed_review_files)
   413→        ]
   414→        concerns_batch = _batch_concerns(
   415→            concerns,
   416→            max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   417→            active_dimensions=dims,
   418→        )
   419→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   420→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   421→        concerns_batch = None
   422→    if concerns_batch:
   423→        batches.append(concerns_batch)
   424→
   425→    batches = _filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   426→        batches,
   427→        dims,
   428→        fallback_max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   429→    )
   430→    include_full_sweep = bool(resolved_options.include_full_sweep)
   431→    # Explicitly scoped dimension runs should stay scoped by default.
   432→    if resolved_options.dimensions:
   433→        include_full_sweep = False
   434→    if include_full_sweep:
   435→        append_full_sweep_batch(
   436→            batches=batches,
   437→            dims=dims,
   438→            all_files=all_files,
   439→            lang=lang,
   440→            max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   441→        )
   442→    batches = _filter_batches_to_file_scope(
   443→        batches,
   444→        allowed_files=allowed_review_files,
   445→    )
   446→
   447→    # Holistic mode can receive per-file-oriented dimensions via CLI suggestions.
   448→    # Attach whichever prompt definition exists so reviewers always get guidance.
   449→    selected_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   450→    for dim in dims:
   451→        prompt = holistic_prompts.get(dim)
   452→        if prompt is None:
   453→            prompt = per_file_prompts.get(dim)
   454→        if prompt is None:
   455→            continue
   456→        selected_prompts[dim] = prompt
   457→
   458→    payload = {
   459→        "command": "review",
   460→        "mode": "holistic",
   461→        "language": lang.name,
   462→        "dimensions": dims,
   463→        "dimension_prompts": selected_prompts,
   464→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   465→        "holistic_context": context.to_dict(),
   466→        "review_context": serialize_context(review_ctx),
   467→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   468→        "total_files": context.codebase_stats.get("total_files", 0),
   469→        "workflow": HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW,
   470→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   471→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   472→            "default": invalid_default,
   473→        },
   474→    }
   475→    if resolved_options.include_issue_history:
   476→        history_payload = build_issue_history_context(
   477→            state,
   478→            options=ReviewHistoryOptions(
   479→                max_issues=resolved_options.issue_history_max_issues,
   480→            ),
   481→        )
   482→        payload["historical_review_issues"] = history_payload
   483→        for batch in batches:
   484→            if not isinstance(batch, dict):
   485→                continue
   486→            batch_dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   487→            batch["historical_issue_focus"] = build_batch_issue_focus(
   488→                history_payload,
   489→                dimensions=batch_dims,
   490→                max_items=resolved_options.issue_history_max_batch_items,
   491→            )
   492→        batches = _filter_batches_to_file_scope(
   493→            batches,
   494→            allowed_files=allowed_review_files,
   495→        )
   496→    payload["investigation_batches"] = batches
   497→    return payload
   498→

1→"""File selection and staleness tracking for review."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→import logging
     7→import re
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import Any
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core.discovery.api import read_file_text
    14→from desloppify.core.pathing.api import rel
    15→from desloppify.intelligence.review.context import (
    16→    abs_path,
    17→    dep_graph_lookup,
    18→    importer_count,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.intelligence.review.selection_cache import (
    21→    count_fresh,
    22→    count_stale,
    23→    get_file_findings,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→
    29→
    30→# Files with these name patterns have low subjective review value —
    31→# they're mostly declarations (types, constants, enums) not logic.
    32→LOW_VALUE_NAMES = re.compile(r"(?:^|/)(?:types|constants|enums|index)\.[a-z]+$")
    33→# Minimum LOC to be worth a review slot.
    34→MIN_REVIEW_LOC = 20
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class ReviewSelectionOptions:
    39→    """Configuration for review file selection."""
    40→
    41→    max_files: int | None = None
    42→    max_age_days: int = 30
    43→    force_refresh: bool = True
    44→    files: list[str] | None = None
    45→
    46→
    47→def hash_file(filepath: str) -> str:
    48→    """Compute a content hash for a file."""
    49→    try:
    50→        content = Path(filepath).read_bytes()
    51→        return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()[:16]
    52→    except OSError:
    53→        return ""
    54→
    55→
    56→def select_files_for_review(
    57→    lang: Any,
    58→    path: Path,
    59→    state: dict,
    60→    options: ReviewSelectionOptions | None = None,
    61→) -> list[str]:
    62→    """Select production files for review, priority-sorted.
    63→
    64→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks).
    65→    """
    66→    resolved_options = options or ReviewSelectionOptions()
    67→
    68→    files = resolved_options.files
    69→    if files is None:
    70→        files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
    71→
    72→    cache = state.get("review_cache", {}).get("files", {})
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    candidates = []
    75→
    76→    for filepath in files:
    77→        rpath = rel(filepath)
    78→
    79→        # Skip non-production files
    80→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
    81→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath)
    82→            if zone.value in ("test", "generated", "vendor"):
    83→                continue
    84→
    85→        # Skip if cached, content unchanged, and not stale
    86→        if not resolved_options.force_refresh:
    87→            entry = cache.get(rpath)
    88→            if entry:
    89→                current_hash = hash_file(abs_path(filepath))
    90→                if current_hash and current_hash == entry.get("content_hash"):
    91→                    reviewed_at = entry.get("reviewed_at", "")
    92→                    if reviewed_at:
    93→                        try:
    94→                            reviewed = datetime.fromisoformat(reviewed_at)
    95→                            age_days = (now - reviewed).days
    96→                            if age_days <= resolved_options.max_age_days:
    97→                                continue  # Still fresh
    98→                        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    99→                            entry["reviewed_at"] = ""
   100→                            logger.debug(
   101→                                "Invalid reviewed_at value %r for %s: %s",
   102→                                reviewed_at,
   103→                                rpath,
   104→                                exc,
   105→                            )
   106→
   107→        priority = _compute_review_priority(filepath, lang, state)
   108→        if priority >= 0:  # Negative = filtered out (too small)
   109→            candidates.append((filepath, priority))
   110→
   111→    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   112→    selected = [f for f, _ in candidates]
   113→    if resolved_options.max_files is None:
   114→        return selected
   115→    return selected[: resolved_options.max_files]
   116→
   117→
   118→def _compute_review_priority(filepath: str, lang, state: dict) -> int:
   119→    """Higher = more important to review.
   120→
   121→    Prioritizes implementation files with high blast radius and existing findings.
   122→    Deprioritizes types/constants files (low subjective review value).
   123→    """
   124→    score = 0
   125→    rpath = rel(filepath)
   126→
   127→    content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   128→    loc = len(content.splitlines()) if content is not None else 0
   129→
   130→    # Skip tiny files — not enough to review
   131→    if loc < MIN_REVIEW_LOC:
   132→        return -1
   133→
   134→    # Low-value files: language-provided pattern or generic fallback.
   135→    is_low_value = is_low_value_file(rpath, lang)
   136→
   137→    # High blast radius (many importers)
   138→    if lang.dep_graph:
   139→        entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   140→        ic = importer_count(entry)
   141→        if is_low_value:
   142→            score += ic * 2
   143→        else:
   144→            score += ic * 10
   145→
   146→    # Already has programmatic findings (compound value — review will be richer)
   147→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   148→    n_findings = sum(
   149→        1 for f in findings.values() if f.get("file") == rpath and f["status"] == "open"
   150→    )
   151→    score += n_findings * 5
   152→
   153→    # High-complexity files with wontfixed structural findings
   154→    # (mechanical detector says "complex" but can't say why — subjective review can)
   155→    n_wontfix_structural = sum(
   156→        1
   157→        for f in findings.values()
   158→        if f.get("file") == rpath
   159→        and f["status"] == "wontfix"
   160→        and f.get("detector") in ("structural", "smells")
   161→    )
   162→    if n_wontfix_structural:
   163→        score += n_wontfix_structural * 15  # Strong boost — these need human insight
   164→
   165→    # Complexity score from mechanical detectors (if available)
   166→    complexity_map = getattr(lang, "complexity_map", None)
   167→    if isinstance(complexity_map, dict) and complexity_map.get(rpath, 0) > 100:
   168→        score += 20  # Very complex files need subjective review most
   169→
   170→    # Larger files have more to review
   171→    score += loc // 50
   172→
   173→    # Low-value penalty — push toward bottom but don't exclude entirely
   174→    if is_low_value:
   175→        score = score // 3
   176→
   177→    return score
   178→
   179→
   180→def low_value_pattern(lang_or_name: Any = None) -> re.Pattern[str]:
   181→    """Return the low-value filename regex for a language, with generic fallback."""
   182→    if lang_or_name is not None and hasattr(lang_or_name, "review_low_value_pattern"):
   183→        pattern = getattr(lang_or_name, "review_low_value_pattern", None)
   184→        if isinstance(pattern, re.Pattern):
   185→            return pattern
   186→
   187→    if isinstance(lang_or_name, str):
   188→        try:
   189→            pattern = getattr(
   190→                get_lang(lang_or_name), "review_low_value_pattern", None
   191→            )
   192→            if isinstance(pattern, re.Pattern):
   193→                return pattern
   194→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
   195→            pattern = None
   196→            logger.debug(
   197→                "Unable to load low-value review pattern for %s: %s", lang_or_name, exc
   198→            )
   199→
   200→    return LOW_VALUE_NAMES
   201→
   202→
   203→def is_low_value_file(filepath: str, lang_or_name=None) -> bool:
   204→    """Whether a file path is low-value for subjective review."""
   205→    pattern = low_value_pattern(lang_or_name)
   206→    return bool(pattern.search(filepath))
   207→
   208→
   209→__all__ = [
   210→    "LOW_VALUE_NAMES",
   211→    "MIN_REVIEW_LOC",
   212→    "ReviewSelectionOptions",
   213→    "count_fresh",
   214→    "count_stale",
   215→    "get_file_findings",
   216→    "hash_file",
   217→    "is_low_value_file",
   218→    "low_value_pattern",
   219→    "select_files_for_review",
   220→]
   221→

1→"""Holistic investigation batch builders for review preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import HolisticContext
     8→
     9→_EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES = {
    10→    "makefile",
    11→    "dockerfile",
    12→    "readme",
    13→    "license",
    14→    "build",
    15→    "workspace",
    16→}
    17→
    18→_GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    19→    "README.md",
    20→    "DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md",
    21→    "desloppify/README.md",
    22→    "pyproject.toml",
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _normalize_file_path(value: object) -> str | None:
    27→    """Normalize/validate candidate file paths for batch payloads."""
    28→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    29→        return None
    30→    text = value.strip().strip(",'\"")
    31→    if not text or text in {".", ".."}:
    32→        return None
    33→    if text.endswith("/"):
    34→        return None
    35→
    36→    basename = Path(text).name
    37→    if not basename:
    38→        return None
    39→    if "." not in basename and basename.lower() not in _EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES:
    40→        return None
    41→    return text
    42→
    43→
    44→def _collect_unique_files(
    45→    sources: list[list[dict]],
    46→    key: str = "file",
    47→    *,
    48→    max_files: int | None = None,
    49→) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Collect unique file paths from multiple source lists."""
    51→    seen: set[str] = set()
    52→    out: list[str] = []
    53→    for src in sources:
    54→        for item in src:
    55→            f = _normalize_file_path(item.get(key, ""))
    56→            if f and f not in seen:
    57→                seen.add(f)
    58→                out.append(f)
    59→                if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    60→                    return out
    61→    return out
    62→
    63→
    64→def _existing_repo_files(
    65→    repo_root: Path | None,
    66→    candidates: tuple[str, ...],
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Return repository-relative paths for candidate files that exist."""
    69→    if repo_root is None:
    70→        return []
    71→    out: list[str] = []
    72→    for candidate in candidates:
    73→        if (repo_root / candidate).is_file():
    74→            out.append(candidate)
    75→    return out
    76→
    77→
    78→def _collect_files_from_batches(
    79→    batches: list[dict], *, max_files: int | None = None
    80→) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Collect unique file paths across batch payloads (preserving order)."""
    82→    seen: set[str] = set()
    83→    out: list[str] = []
    84→    for batch in batches:
    85→        for filepath in batch.get("files_to_read", []):
    86→            normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
    87→            if not normalized:
    88→                continue
    89→            if normalized in seen:
    90→                continue
    91→            seen.add(normalized)
    92→            out.append(normalized)
    93→            if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    94→                return out
    95→    return out
    96→
    97→
    98→def _representative_files_for_directory(
    99→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   100→    directory: str,
   101→    *,
   102→    max_files: int = 3,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Map a directory-level signal to representative file paths."""
   105→    if not isinstance(directory, str) or not directory.strip():
   106→        return []
   107→
   108→    dir_key = directory.strip()
   109→    if dir_key in {".", "./"}:
   110→        normalized_dir = "."
   111→    else:
   112→        normalized_dir = f"{dir_key.rstrip('/')}/"
   113→
   114→    profiles = ctx.structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   115→    profile = profiles.get(normalized_dir)
   116→    if not isinstance(profile, dict):
   117→        return []
   118→
   119→    out: list[str] = []
   120→    for filename in profile.get("files", []):
   121→        if not isinstance(filename, str) or not filename:
   122→            continue
   123→        filepath = (
   124→            filename
   125→            if normalized_dir == "."
   126→            else f"{normalized_dir.rstrip('/')}/{filename}"
   127→        )
   128→        normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
   129→        if not normalized or normalized in out:
   130→            continue
   131→        out.append(normalized)
   132→        if len(out) >= max_files:
   133→            break
   134→    return out
   135→
   136→
   137→def _batch_arch_coupling(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   138→    """Batch 1: Architecture & Coupling - god modules, import-time side effects."""
   139→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   140→        [
   141→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   142→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   143→            ctx.coupling.get("boundary_violations", []),
   144→            ctx.dependencies.get("deferred_import_density", []),
   145→        ],
   146→        max_files=max_files,
   147→    )
   148→    return {
   149→        "name": "Architecture & Coupling",
   150→        "dimensions": ["cross_module_architecture", "high_level_elegance"],
   151→        "files_to_read": files,
   152→        "why": "god modules, import-time side effects, boundary violations, deferred import pressure",
   153→    }
   154→
   155→
   156→def _batch_conventions_errors(
   157→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   158→) -> dict:
   159→    """Batch 2: Conventions & Errors - sibling behavior outliers, mixed strategies."""
   160→    sibling = ctx.conventions.get("sibling_behavior", {})
   161→    outlier_files = [
   162→        {"file": o["file"]} for di in sibling.values() for o in di.get("outliers", [])
   163→    ]
   164→    error_dirs = ctx.errors.get("strategy_by_directory", {})
   165→    mixed_dir_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   166→    for directory, strategies in error_dirs.items():
   167→        if not isinstance(strategies, dict) or len(strategies) < 3:
   168→            continue
   169→        for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   170→            mixed_dir_files.append({"file": filepath})
   171→
   172→    exception_files = [
   173→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   174→        for item in ctx.errors.get("exception_hotspots", [])
   175→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   176→    ]
   177→    dupe_files = [
   178→        {"file": item.get("files", [""])[0]}
   179→        for item in ctx.conventions.get("duplicate_clusters", [])
   180→        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("files")
   181→    ]
   182→    naming_drift_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   183→    for entry in ctx.conventions.get("naming_drift", []):
   184→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   185→            directory = entry.get("directory", "")
   186→            for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   187→                naming_drift_files.append({"file": filepath})
   188→
   189→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   190→        [outlier_files, mixed_dir_files, exception_files, dupe_files, naming_drift_files],
   191→        max_files=max_files,
   192→    )
   193→    return {
   194→        "name": "Conventions & Errors",
   195→        "dimensions": ["convention_outlier", "error_consistency", "mid_level_elegance"],
   196→        "files_to_read": files,
   197→        "why": "naming drift, behavioral outliers, mixed error strategies, exception hotspots, duplicate clusters",
   198→    }
   199→
   200→
   201→def _batch_abstractions_deps(
   202→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   203→) -> dict:
   204→    """Batch 3: Abstractions & Dependencies - abstraction hotspots, dep cycles."""
   205→    util_files = ctx.abstractions.get("util_files", [])
   206→    wrapper_files = [
   207→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   208→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("pass_through_wrappers", [])
   209→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   210→    ]
   211→    indirection_files = [
   212→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   213→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("indirection_hotspots", [])
   214→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   215→    ]
   216→    param_bag_files = [
   217→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   218→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("wide_param_bags", [])
   219→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   220→    ]
   221→    interface_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   222→    for item in ctx.abstractions.get("one_impl_interfaces", []):
   223→        if not isinstance(item, dict):
   224→            continue
   225→        for group in ("declared_in", "implemented_in"):
   226→            for filepath in item.get(group, []):
   227→                interface_files.append({"file": filepath})
   228→
   229→    delegation_files = [
   230→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   231→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("delegation_heavy_classes", [])
   232→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   233→    ]
   234→    facade_files = [
   235→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   236→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("facade_modules", [])
   237→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   238→    ]
   239→    type_violation_files = [
   240→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   241→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("typed_dict_violations", [])
   242→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   243→    ]
   244→
   245→    complexity_files = [
   246→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   247→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("complexity_hotspots", [])
   248→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   249→    ]
   250→
   251→    cycle_files = [
   252→        {"file": path}
   253→        for path in ctx.dependencies.get("cycle_files", [])
   254→        if isinstance(path, str) and path.strip()
   255→    ]
   256→    if not cycle_files:
   257→        # Backward-compatible fallback for older packets that only carry summaries.
   258→        for summary in ctx.dependencies.get("cycle_summaries", []):
   259→            if not isinstance(summary, str):
   260→                continue
   261→            for token in summary.split():
   262→                if "/" in token and "." in token:
   263→                    cycle_files.append({"file": token.strip(",'\"")})
   264→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   265→        [
   266→            util_files,
   267→            wrapper_files,
   268→            indirection_files,
   269→            param_bag_files,
   270→            interface_files,
   271→            delegation_files,
   272→            facade_files,
   273→            type_violation_files,
   274→            complexity_files,
   275→            cycle_files,
   276→        ],
   277→        max_files=max_files,
   278→    )
   279→    return {
   280→        "name": "Abstractions & Dependencies",
   281→        "dimensions": [
   282→            "abstraction_fitness",
   283→            "dependency_health",
   284→            "mid_level_elegance",
   285→            "low_level_elegance",
   286→        ],
   287→        "files_to_read": files,
   288→        "why": "abstraction hotspots (wrappers/interfaces/param bags/delegation-heavy classes/facade modules/TypedDict violations), dep cycles",
   289→    }
   290→
   291→
   292→def _batch_testing_api(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   293→    """Batch 4: Testing & API - critical untested paths, sync/async mix."""
   294→    critical = ctx.testing.get("critical_untested", [])
   295→    sync_async = [{"file": f} for f in ctx.api_surface.get("sync_async_mix", [])]
   296→    files = _collect_unique_files([critical, sync_async], max_files=max_files)
   297→    return {
   298→        "name": "Testing & API",
   299→        "dimensions": ["test_strategy", "api_surface_coherence", "mid_level_elegance"],
   300→        "files_to_read": files,
   301→        "why": "critical untested paths, API inconsistency",
   302→    }

   304→
   305→def _batch_authorization(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   306→    """Batch 5: Authorization - auth gaps, service role usage."""
   307→    auth_ctx = ctx.authorization
   308→    auth_files: list[dict] = []
   309→    for rpath, info in auth_ctx.get("route_auth_coverage", {}).items():
   310→        if info.get("without_auth", 0) > 0:
   311→            auth_files.append({"file": rpath})
   312→    for rpath in auth_ctx.get("service_role_usage", []):
   313→        auth_files.append({"file": rpath})
   314→    files = _collect_unique_files([auth_files], max_files=max_files)
   315→    return {
   316→        "name": "Authorization",
   317→        "dimensions": ["authorization_consistency", "mid_level_elegance"],
   318→        "files_to_read": files,
   319→        "why": "auth gaps, service role usage, RLS coverage",
   320→    }

   322→
   323→def _batch_ai_debt_migrations(
   324→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   325→) -> dict:
   326→    """Batch 6: AI Debt & Migrations - deprecated markers, migration TODOs."""
   327→    ai_debt = ctx.ai_debt_signals
   328→    migration = ctx.migration_signals
   329→    debt_files: list[dict] = []
   330→    for rpath in ai_debt.get("file_signals", {}):
   331→        debt_files.append({"file": rpath})
   332→    dep_files = migration.get("deprecated_markers", {}).get("files")
   333→    if isinstance(dep_files, dict):
   334→        for entry in dep_files:
   335→            debt_files.append({"file": entry})
   336→    for entry in migration.get("migration_todos", []):
   337→        debt_files.append({"file": entry.get("file", "")})
   338→    files = _collect_unique_files([debt_files], max_files=max_files)
   339→    return {
   340→        "name": "AI Debt & Migrations",
   341→        "dimensions": [
   342→            "ai_generated_debt",
   343→            "incomplete_migration",
   344→            "low_level_elegance",
   345→        ],
   346→        "files_to_read": files,
   347→        "why": "AI-generated patterns, deprecated markers, migration TODOs",
   348→    }
   349→
   350→
   351→def _batch_package_organization(
   352→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   353→) -> dict:
   354→    """Batch 7: Package Organization - file placement, directory boundaries."""
   355→    structure = ctx.structure
   356→    struct_files: list[dict] = []
   357→    # Add flat_dir_findings directory representatives
   358→    for entry in structure.get("flat_dir_findings", []):
   359→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   360→            directory = entry.get("directory", "")
   361→            for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   362→                struct_files.append({"file": filepath})
   363→    for rf in structure.get("root_files", []):
   364→        if rf.get("role") == "peripheral":
   365→            struct_files.append({"file": rf["file"]})
   366→    dir_profiles = structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   367→    largest_dirs = sorted(
   368→        dir_profiles.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1].get("file_count", 0)
   369→    )[:3]
   370→    for dir_key, profile in largest_dirs:
   371→        for fname in profile.get("files", [])[:3]:
   372→            dir_path = dir_key.rstrip("/")
   373→            rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   374→            struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   375→    coupling_matrix = structure.get("coupling_matrix", {})
   376→    seen_edges: set[str] = set()
   377→    for edge in coupling_matrix:
   378→        if " → " in edge:
   379→            a, b = edge.split(" → ", 1)
   380→            reverse = f"{b} → {a}"
   381→            if reverse in coupling_matrix and edge not in seen_edges:
   382→                seen_edges.add(edge)
   383→                seen_edges.add(reverse)
   384→                for d in (a, b):
   385→                    for fname in dir_profiles.get(d, {}).get("files", [])[:2]:
   386→                        dir_path = d.rstrip("/")
   387→                        rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   388→                        struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   389→    files = _collect_unique_files([struct_files], max_files=max_files)
   390→    return {
   391→        "name": "Package Organization",
   392→        "dimensions": ["package_organization", "high_level_elegance"],
   393→        "files_to_read": files,
   394→        "why": "file placement, directory boundaries, architectural layering",
   395→    }
   396→
   397→
   398→def _batch_state_design(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   399→    """Batch 8: State & Design Integrity - mutable globals, signal density hotspots."""
   400→    evidence = ctx.scan_evidence
   401→    mutable_files = [
   402→        item for item in evidence.get("mutable_globals", [])
   403→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   404→    ]
   405→    complexity_files = [
   406→        item for item in evidence.get("complexity_hotspots", [])[:10]
   407→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   408→    ]
   409→    error_files = [
   410→        item for item in evidence.get("error_hotspots", [])[:10]
   411→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   412→    ]
   413→    density_files = [
   414→        {"file": item["file"]}
   415→        for item in evidence.get("signal_density", [])[:10]
   416→        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("file")
   417→    ]
   418→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   419→        [mutable_files, complexity_files, error_files, density_files],
   420→        max_files=max_files,
   421→    )
   422→    return {
   423→        "name": "State & Design Integrity",
   424→        "dimensions": ["initialization_coupling", "design_coherence"],
   425→        "files_to_read": files,
   426→        "why": "mutable global state, concentrated quality signals, initialization coupling patterns",
   427→    }

   429→
   430→def _batch_governance_contracts(
   431→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   432→    *,
   433→    repo_root: Path | None,
   434→    max_files: int | None = None,
   435→) -> dict:
   436→    """Batch 8: Governance & Contracts - docs/policy promises vs runtime posture."""
   437→    docs = _existing_repo_files(repo_root, _GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES)
   438→    if not docs:
   439→        return {
   440→            "name": "Governance & Contracts",
   441→            "dimensions": [
   442→                "cross_module_architecture",
   443→                "high_level_elegance",
   444→                "test_strategy",
   445→                "package_organization",
   446→            ],
   447→            "files_to_read": [],
   448→            "why": "architecture contracts, compatibility policy, docs-vs-runtime scope, and quality-gate coverage",
   449→        }
   450→    top_imported = [
   451→        {"file": filepath}
   452→        for filepath in list(ctx.architecture.get("top_imported", {}).keys())[:5]
   453→        if isinstance(filepath, str)
   454→    ]
   455→    anchor_files = _collect_unique_files(
   456→        [
   457→            top_imported,
   458→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   459→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   460→        ],
   461→        max_files=5,
   462→    )
   463→    seen = set(docs)
   464→    files = list(docs)
   465→    for filepath in anchor_files:
   466→        if filepath in seen:
   467→            continue
   468→        seen.add(filepath)
   469→        files.append(filepath)
   470→    if max_files is not None:
   471→        files = files[:max_files]
   472→    return {
   473→        "name": "Governance & Contracts",
   474→        "dimensions": [
   475→            "cross_module_architecture",
   476→            "high_level_elegance",
   477→            "test_strategy",
   478→            "package_organization",
   479→        ],
   480→        "files_to_read": files,
   481→        "why": "architecture contracts, compatibility policy, docs-vs-runtime scope, and quality-gate coverage",
   482→    }
   483→
   484→
   485→def _ensure_holistic_context(holistic_ctx: HolisticContext | dict) -> HolisticContext:
   486→    if isinstance(holistic_ctx, HolisticContext):
   487→        return holistic_ctx
   488→    return HolisticContext.from_raw(holistic_ctx)
   489→
   490→
   491→def build_investigation_batches(
   492→    holistic_ctx: HolisticContext | dict,
   493→    lang: object,
   494→    *,
   495→    repo_root: Path | None = None,
   496→    max_files_per_batch: int | None = None,
   497→) -> list[dict]:
   498→    """Derive parallelizable investigation batches from holistic context."""
   499→    ctx = _ensure_holistic_context(holistic_ctx)
   500→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific batch shaping.
   501→    batches = [
   502→        _batch_arch_coupling(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   503→        _batch_conventions_errors(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   504→        _batch_abstractions_deps(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   505→        _batch_testing_api(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   506→        _batch_authorization(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   507→        _batch_ai_debt_migrations(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   508→        _batch_package_organization(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   509→        _batch_state_design(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   510→        _batch_governance_contracts(
   511→            ctx,
   512→            repo_root=repo_root,
   513→            max_files=max_files_per_batch,
   514→        ),
   515→    ]
   516→    return [batch for batch in batches if batch["files_to_read"]]
   517→
   518→
   519→def filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   520→    batches: list[dict],
   521→    dimensions: list[str],
   522→    *,
   523→    fallback_max_files: int | None = 80,
   524→) -> list[dict]:
   525→    """Keep only dimensions explicitly active for this holistic review run.
   526→
   527→    If selected dimensions are not represented by any batch mapping, append a
   528→    fallback batch over representative files so scoped runs still get guidance.
   529→    """
   530→    selected = [d for d in dimensions if isinstance(d, str) and d]
   531→    if not selected:
   532→        return []
   533→    selected_set = set(selected)
   534→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   535→    covered: set[str] = set()
   536→    for batch in batches:
   537→        batch_dims = [dim for dim in batch.get("dimensions", []) if dim in selected_set]
   538→        if not batch_dims:
   539→            continue
   540→        filtered.append({**batch, "dimensions": batch_dims})
   541→        covered.update(batch_dims)
   542→
   543→    missing = [dim for dim in selected if dim not in covered]
   544→    if not missing:
   545→        return filtered
   546→
   547→    # Keep fallback batches tractable; giant sweeps are expensive and often
   548→    # unnecessary when dimensions are already explicitly scoped.
   549→    max_files = fallback_max_files if isinstance(fallback_max_files, int) else None
   550→    if isinstance(max_files, int) and max_files <= 0:
   551→        max_files = None
   552→    fallback_files = _collect_files_from_batches(filtered or batches, max_files=max_files)
   553→    if not fallback_files:
   554→        return filtered
   555→
   556→    filtered.append(
   557→        {
   558→            "name": "Cross-cutting Sweep",
   559→            "dimensions": missing,
   560→            "files_to_read": fallback_files,
   561→            "why": "selected dimensions had no direct batch mapping; review representative cross-cutting files",
   562→        }
   563→    )
   564→    return filtered
   565→
   566→
   567→def batch_concerns(
   568→    concerns: list,
   569→    *,
   570→    max_files: int | None = None,
   571→    active_dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
   572→) -> dict | None:
   573→    """Build investigation batch from mechanical concern signals.
   574→
   575→    *concerns* should be a list of Concern dataclass instances from
   576→    ``desloppify.engine.concerns``.
   577→    """
   578→    if not concerns:
   579→        return None
   580→    default_dims = ["design_coherence", "initialization_coupling"]
   581→    selected_dims = [
   582→        dim for dim in (active_dimensions or [])
   583→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim
   584→    ]
   585→    selected_set = set(selected_dims)
   586→    overlap_dims = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim in selected_set]
   587→    concern_dims = overlap_dims or list(default_dims)
   588→    mapped_to_active_dims = bool(selected_dims) and not overlap_dims
   589→    if mapped_to_active_dims:
   590→        # Prevent concern signals from being silently dropped by scoped runs.
   591→        concern_dims = list(selected_dims)
   592→
   593→    types = sorted({c.type for c in concerns if c.type})
   594→    why_parts = ["mechanical detectors identified structural patterns needing judgment"]
   595→    if types:
   596→        why_parts.append(f"concern types: {', '.join(types)}")
   597→    if mapped_to_active_dims:
   598→        why_parts.append(
   599→            "mapped to active dimensions because design_coherence/initialization_coupling are not selected"
   600→        )
   601→    files: list[str] = []
   602→    seen: set[str] = set()
   603→    concern_signals: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   604→    for concern in concerns:
   605→        candidate = _normalize_file_path(getattr(concern, "file", ""))
   606→        if not candidate or candidate in seen:
   607→            continue
   608→        seen.add(candidate)
   609→        files.append(candidate)
   610→
   611→        evidence_raw = getattr(concern, "evidence", ())
   612→        evidence = [
   613→            str(entry).strip()
   614→            for entry in evidence_raw
   615→            if isinstance(entry, str) and entry.strip()
   616→        ][:4]
   617→        summary = str(getattr(concern, "summary", "")).strip()
   618→        question = str(getattr(concern, "question", "")).strip()
   619→        concern_type = str(getattr(concern, "type", "")).strip()
   620→        concern_signals.append(
   621→            {
   622→                "type": concern_type or "design_concern",
   623→                "file": candidate,
   624→                "summary": summary or "Mechanical concern requires subjective judgment",
   625→                "question": question or "Is this pattern intentional or debt?",
   626→                "evidence": evidence,
   627→            }
   628→        )
   629→
   630→    total_candidate_files = len(files)
   631→    if (
   632→        max_files is not None
   633→        and isinstance(max_files, int)
   634→        and max_files > 0
   635→        and total_candidate_files > max_files
   636→    ):
   637→        files = files[:max_files]
   638→        why_parts.append(
   639→            f"truncated to {max_files} files from {total_candidate_files} candidates"
   640→        )
   641→
   642→    return {
   643→        "name": "Design coherence — Mechanical Concern Signals",
   644→        "dimensions": concern_dims,
   645→        "files_to_read": files,
   646→        "why": "; ".join(why_parts),
   647→        "total_candidate_files": total_candidate_files,
   648→        "concern_signals": concern_signals[:12],
   649→        "concern_signal_count": len(concern_signals),
   650→        "mapped_to_active_dimensions": mapped_to_active_dims,
   651→    }
   652→
   653→
   654→__all__ = ["batch_concerns", "build_investigation_batches", "filter_batches_to_dimensions"]
   655→

1→"""Load and validate review dimension payloads from JSON data files."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→import json
     7→from functools import lru_cache
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import ensure_prompt_contract
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.validation import (
    12→    parse_dimensions_payload,
    13→)
    14→
    15→_LANG_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "languages"
    16→_LANG_DATA_SUBDIR = "review_data"
    17→_DATA_DIR = _LANG_DIR / "_framework" / _LANG_DATA_SUBDIR
    18→
    19→# Canonical filename for the unified dimensions payload.
    20→_DIMENSIONS_FILE = "dimensions.json"
    21→
    22→
    23→def _load_json_payload_from_path(path: Path) -> dict:
    24→    """Load a JSON payload from *path* and return a dict."""
    25→    try:
    26→        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    27→    except OSError as exc:
    28→        raise RuntimeError(f"Unable to read dimensions payload: {path}") from exc
    29→
    30→    try:
    31→        payload = json.loads(raw)
    32→    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
    33→        raise ValueError(f"Invalid JSON in dimensions payload: {path}") from exc
    34→
    35→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    36→        raise ValueError(f"Dimensions payload must be a JSON object: {path}")
    37→    return payload
    38→
    39→
    40→def _lang_payload_path(lang_name: str, filename: str) -> Path:
    41→    """Resolve the per-language review payload path."""
    42→    return _LANG_DIR / lang_name / _LANG_DATA_SUBDIR / filename
    43→
    44→
    45→def _override_filename(filename: str) -> str:
    46→    """Convert ``foo.json`` to ``foo.override.json``."""
    47→    if not filename.endswith(".json"):
    48→        return f"{filename}.override.json"
    49→    return f"{filename[:-5]}.override.json"
    50→
    51→
    52→def _load_json_payload(filename: str) -> dict:
    53→    """Load a JSON payload from the shared language review-data directory."""
    54→    return _load_json_payload_from_path(_DATA_DIR / filename)
    55→
    56→
    57→def _validate_optional_string_list(value: object, *, context: str) -> list[str]:
    58→    """Validate an optional list of strings (empty allowed)."""
    59→    if value is None:
    60→        return []
    61→    if not isinstance(value, list):
    62→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be a list of strings")
    63→    out: list[str] = []
    64→    for idx, item in enumerate(value):
    65→        if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():
    66→            raise ValueError(f"{context}[{idx}] must be a non-empty string")
    67→        out.append(item)
    68→    return out
    69→
    70→
    71→def _validate_override_keys(
    72→    override_payload: dict,
    73→    *,
    74→    dims_key: str,
    75→    context: str,
    76→) -> None:
    77→    """Validate allowed top-level keys in dimensions override payload."""
    78→    allowed = {
    79→        dims_key,
    80→        f"{dims_key}_append",
    81→        f"{dims_key}_remove",
    82→        "dimension_prompts",
    83→        "dimension_prompts_remove",
    84→        "system_prompt",
    85→        "system_prompt_append",
    86→    }
    87→    actual = set(override_payload)
    88→    extra = sorted(actual - allowed)
    89→    if extra:
    90→        raise ValueError(f"{context} has unsupported keys: {extra}")
    91→
    92→
    93→def _apply_dimension_list_override(
    94→    out: dict,
    95→    override_payload: dict,
    96→    *,
    97→    dims_key: str,
    98→    context: str,
    99→) -> None:
   100→    """Apply replace/append/remove operations for the dimension list."""
   101→    if dims_key in override_payload:
   102→        out[dims_key] = override_payload[dims_key]
   103→
   104→    dims = list(out.get(dims_key, []))
   105→    append_dims = _validate_optional_string_list(
   106→        override_payload.get(f"{dims_key}_append"),
   107→        context=f"{context}.{dims_key}_append",
   108→    )
   109→    for dim in append_dims:
   110→        if dim not in dims:
   111→            dims.append(dim)
   112→
   113→    remove_dims = set(
   114→        _validate_optional_string_list(
   115→            override_payload.get(f"{dims_key}_remove"),
   116→            context=f"{context}.{dims_key}_remove",
   117→        )
   118→    )
   119→    if remove_dims:
   120→        dims = [dim for dim in dims if dim not in remove_dims]
   121→    out[dims_key] = dims
   122→
   123→
   124→def _apply_dimension_prompt_override(
   125→    out: dict,
   126→    override_payload: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    context: str,
   129→) -> None:
   130→    """Apply prompt add/update/remove override operations."""
   131→    if "dimension_prompts" in override_payload:
   132→        prompt_overrides = override_payload["dimension_prompts"]
   133→        if not isinstance(prompt_overrides, dict):
   134→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.dimension_prompts must be an object")
   135→        prompts = dict(out.get("dimension_prompts", {}))
   136→        for dim_name, prompt in prompt_overrides.items():
   137→            prompts[dim_name] = prompt
   138→        out["dimension_prompts"] = prompts
   139→
   140→    remove_prompts = _validate_optional_string_list(
   141→        override_payload.get("dimension_prompts_remove"),
   142→        context=f"{context}.dimension_prompts_remove",
   143→    )
   144→    if remove_prompts:
   145→        prompts = dict(out.get("dimension_prompts", {}))
   146→        for dim_name in remove_prompts:
   147→            prompts.pop(dim_name, None)
   148→        out["dimension_prompts"] = prompts
   149→
   150→
   151→def _apply_system_prompt_override(
   152→    out: dict,
   153→    override_payload: dict,
   154→    *,
   155→    context: str,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Apply system prompt replace/append override operations."""
   158→    if "system_prompt" in override_payload:
   159→        out["system_prompt"] = override_payload["system_prompt"]
   160→    if "system_prompt_append" not in override_payload:
   161→        return
   162→    suffix = override_payload["system_prompt_append"]
   163→    if not isinstance(suffix, str):
   164→        raise ValueError(f"{context}.system_prompt_append must be a string")
   165→    current = out.get("system_prompt", "")
   166→    sep = "\n\n" if current and suffix else ""
   167→    out["system_prompt"] = f"{current}{sep}{suffix}"
   168→
   169→
   170→def _apply_dimensions_override(
   171→    base_payload: dict,
   172→    override_payload: dict,
   173→    *,
   174→    dims_key: str,
   175→    context: str,
   176→) -> dict:
   177→    """Apply a language override payload to a base dimensions payload."""
   178→    if not isinstance(override_payload, dict):
   179→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be a JSON object")
   180→
   181→    _validate_override_keys(override_payload, dims_key=dims_key, context=context)
   182→    out = copy.deepcopy(base_payload)
   183→    _apply_dimension_list_override(
   184→        out, override_payload, dims_key=dims_key, context=context
   185→    )
   186→    _apply_dimension_prompt_override(out, override_payload, context=context)
   187→    _apply_system_prompt_override(out, override_payload, context=context)
   188→    return out
   189→
   190→
   191→def _load_payload_for_lang(
   192→    lang_name: str,
   193→    filename: str,
   194→    *,
   195→    dims_key: str,
   196→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   197→    """Load payload for a language using shared-base + optional language overlay.
   198→
   199→    Resolution order:
   200→    1) Start from shared base payload in ``lang/framework/review_data``.
   201→    2) If language override payload exists, patch the selected base.
   202→    """
   203→    base_payload = _load_json_payload(filename)
   204→    context = filename
   205→
   206→    lang_override_path = _lang_payload_path(lang_name, _override_filename(filename))
   207→    if lang_override_path.is_file():
   208→        override_payload = _load_json_payload_from_path(lang_override_path)
   209→        base_payload = _apply_dimensions_override(
   210→            base_payload,
   211→            override_payload,
   212→            dims_key=dims_key,
   213→            context=str(lang_override_path),
   214→        )
   215→        context = f"{context} + {lang_override_path}"
   216→
   217→    return base_payload, context
   218→
   219→
   220→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   221→# Canonical loaders — use these for new code.
   222→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   223→
   224→
   225→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   226→def load_dimensions() -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   227→    """Load and validate the unified review dimension definitions."""
   228→    payload = _load_json_payload(_DIMENSIONS_FILE)
   229→    dims, prompts, system_prompt = parse_dimensions_payload(
   230→        payload, context_prefix=_DIMENSIONS_FILE
   231→    )
   232→    return dims, prompts, ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt)
   233→
   234→
   235→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   236→def load_dimensions_for_lang(
   237→    lang_name: str,
   238→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   239→    """Load unified review dimensions for a language (with lang override applied)."""
   240→    payload, context = _load_payload_for_lang(
   241→        lang_name,
   242→        _DIMENSIONS_FILE,
   243→        [REDACTED],
   244→    )
   245→    dims, prompts, system_prompt = parse_dimensions_payload(
   246→        payload, context_prefix=context
   247→    )
   248→    return dims, prompts, ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt)
   249→
