   124→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   125→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   126→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   127→        )
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    if import_file:
   131→        do_import(
   132→            import_file,
   133→            state,
   134→            lang,
   135→            state_file,
   136→            config=runtime.config,
   137→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   138→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   139→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   140→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   141→            force_review_import=bool(getattr(args, "force_review_import", False)),
   142→        )
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   146→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _try_reconfigure_stream(stream: object) -> bool:
   150→    """Attempt line-buffered reconfigure on a stream, returning success."""
   151→    reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
   152→    if not callable(reconfigure):
   153→        return False
   154→    try:
   155→        reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
   156→    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
   157→        return False
   158→    return True
   159→
   160→
   161→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
   162→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
   163→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
   164→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
   165→        _try_reconfigure_stream(stream)
   166→
   167→
   168→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   169→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
   170→    _enable_live_review_output()
   171→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   172→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   173→    state = runtime.state
   174→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   175→
   176→    if not lang:
   177→        print(
   178→            colorize("  Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", "red"),
   179→            file=sys.stderr,
   180→        )
   181→        sys.exit(1)
   182→
   183→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
   184→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
   185→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
   186→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
   187→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
   188→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
   189→
   190→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
   191→    _validate_mode_selection([
   192→        merge,
   193→        run_batches,
   194→        external_start,
   195→        external_submit,
   196→        import_mode,
   197→        bool(validate_import_file),
   198→    ])
   199→    _validate_external_submit_args(
   200→        external_submit=external_submit,
   201→        import_file=import_file,
   202→        session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
   203→    )
   204→    _dispatch_review_mode(
   205→        args=args,
   206→        runtime=runtime,
   207→        state=state,
   208→        lang=lang,
   209→        state_file=state_file,
   210→        merge=merge,
   211→        run_batches=run_batches,
   212→        external_start=external_start,
   213→        external_submit=external_submit,
   214→        import_file=import_file,
   215→        validate_import_file=validate_import_file,
   216→    )

    21→
    22→def do_prepare(
    23→    args,
    24→    state,
    25→    lang,
    26→    _state_path,
    27→    *,
    28→    config: dict,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Prepare mode: holistic-only review packet in query.json."""

    34→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))

    39→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(

    41→        default=20,
    42→    )
    43→
    44→    lang_run, found_files = review_runtime_mod.setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
    45→
    46→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    47→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    48→        state,
    49→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    50→    )
    51→    data = packet_builder_mod.build_review_packet(
    52→        path=path,
    53→        lang_run=lang_run,
    54→        state=state,
    55→        config=config,
    56→        dimensions=dimensions,
    57→        files=found_files or None,
    58→        narrative=narrative,
    59→        retrospective=retrospective,
    60→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    61→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    62→        include_scan_after_import=True,
    63→    )
    64→    next_command = str(data.get("next_command", ""))
    65→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    66→    if total == 0:
    67→        print(
    68→            colorize(

    70→                "Nothing to review.",
    71→                "red",
    72→            ),
    73→            file=sys.stderr,
    74→        )
    75→        scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if isinstance(state, dict) else None
    76→        if scan_path:
    77→            print(
    78→                colorize(
    79→                    f"  Hint: your last scan used --path {scan_path}. "
    80→                    f"Try: desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}",
    81→                    "yellow",
    82→                ),
    83→                file=sys.stderr,
    84→            )
    85→        else:
    86→            print(
    87→                colorize(
    88→                    "  Hint: pass --path <dir> matching the path used during scan.",
    89→                    "yellow",
    90→                ),
    91→                file=sys.stderr,
    92→            )
    93→        sys.exit(1)
    94→    write_query(data)
    95→    batches = data.get("investigation_batches", [])
    96→    print(colorize(f"\n  Holistic review prepared: {total} files in codebase", "bold"))
    97→    if retrospective:
    98→        print(
    99→            colorize(
   100→                "  Retrospective context enabled: historical review issues injected into packet.",
   101→                "dim",
   102→            )
   103→        )

   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→        for i, batch in enumerate(batches, 1):
   111→            n_files = len(batch["files_to_read"])
   112→            print(
   113→                colorize(
   114→                    f"    {i}. {batch['name']} ({n_files} files) — {batch['why']}",
   115→                    "dim",
   116→                )
   117→            )
   118→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "bold"))
   119→    for step_i, step in enumerate(data.get("workflow", []), 1):
   120→        print(colorize(f"    {step_i}. {step}", "dim"))
   121→    print()
   122→    print_agent_plan(
   123→        review_execution_steps(
   124→            next_command=next_command,
   125→            include_manual_override=True,
   126→            include_scan_after_findings_import=False,
   127→        ),
   128→        next_command=next_command,
   129→        header="  AGENT PLAN:",
   130→    )
   131→    print(
   132→        colorize(
   133→            "  Next command to improve subjective scores: "
   134→            f"`{next_command}`",
   135→            "dim",
   136→        )
   137→    )
   138→    print(

   141→            f"Preferred next step: {next_command}",
   142→            "cyan",
   143→        )
   144→    )

    45→)
    46→ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES = {
    47→    "abstraction_leverage": "Abstraction Leverage",
    48→    "indirection_cost": "Indirection Cost",
    49→    "interface_honesty": "Interface Honesty",
    50→    "delegation_density": "Delegation Density",
    51→    "definition_directness": "Definition Directness",
    52→    "type_discipline": "Type Discipline",
    53→}
    54→
    55→
    56→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    57→class BatchPathContext:
    58→    """Invocation-scoped review batch paths resolved once per command run."""
    59→
    60→    project_root: Path
    61→    review_packet_dir: Path
    62→    subagent_runs_dir: Path
    63→    blind_packet_path: Path
    64→
    65→
    66→def _consume_path_override(name: str) -> Path | None:
    67→    """Consume one compatibility override to prevent cross-command bleed-through."""
    68→    value = globals().get(name)
    69→    if isinstance(value, Path):
    70→        globals()[name] = None
    71→        return value
    72→    return None
    73→
    74→
    75→def _resolve_batch_paths() -> BatchPathContext:
    76→    project_root = _consume_path_override("PROJECT_ROOT") or get_project_root()
    77→    review_packet_dir = (
    78→        _consume_path_override("REVIEW_PACKET_DIR")
    79→        or project_root / ".desloppify" / "review_packets"
    80→    )
    81→    subagent_runs_dir = (
    82→        _consume_path_override("SUBAGENT_RUNS_DIR")
    83→        or project_root / ".desloppify" / "subagents" / "runs"
    84→    )
    85→    blind_packet_path = project_root / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
    86→    return BatchPathContext(
    87→        project_root=project_root,
    88→        review_packet_dir=review_packet_dir,
    89→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
    90→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    95→    """Deterministically merge assessments/findings across batch outputs."""
    96→    normalized_results: list[dict] = []
    97→    for result in batch_results:
    98→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
    99→            payload = result.to_dict()
   100→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   101→                normalized_results.append(payload)
   102→                continue
   103→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   104→            normalized_results.append(result)
   105→    return batch_core_mod.merge_batch_results(
   106→        normalized_results,
   107→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   108→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   109→    )
   110→
   111→
   112→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
   113→    args,
   114→    *,
   115→    state: dict,
   116→    lang,
   117→    config: dict,
   118→    stamp: str,
   119→    paths: BatchPathContext,
   120→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   121→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   122→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   123→    if packet_override:
   124→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   125→        if not packet_path.exists():
   126→            print_error(f"packet not found: {packet_override}")
   127→            sys.exit(1)
   128→        try:
   129→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   130→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   131→            print_error(f"reading packet: {exc}")
   132→            sys.exit(1)
   133→        blind_path = paths.blind_packet_path
   134→        blind_packet = runner_helpers_mod.build_blind_packet(packet)
   135→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   136→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   137→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   138→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   139→
   140→    path = Path(args.path)
   141→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   142→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   143→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   144→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   145→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   146→        default=30,
   147→        minimum=1,
   148→    )
   149→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   150→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   151→        default=20,
   152→        minimum=1,
   153→    )
   154→    lang_run, found_files = _setup_lang(lang, path, config)
   155→    lang_name = lang_run.name
   156→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   157→        state,
   158→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
   159→    )
   160→
   161→    blind_path = paths.blind_packet_path
   162→    packet = packet_builder_mod.build_review_packet(
   163→        path=path,
   164→        lang_run=lang_run,
   165→        state=state,
   166→        config=config,
   167→        dimensions=dimensions,
   168→        files=found_files or None,
   169→        narrative=narrative,
   170→        retrospective=retrospective,
   171→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   172→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   173→        include_scan_after_import=False,
   174→    )
   175→    write_query_best_effort(
   176→        packet,
   177→        context="review packet query update",
   178→    )
   179→    packet_path, blind_saved = runner_helpers_mod.write_packet_snapshot(
   180→        packet,
   181→        stamp=stamp,
   182→        review_packet_dir=paths.review_packet_dir,
   183→        blind_path=blind_path,
   184→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   185→    )
   186→    print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   187→    print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   188→    return packet, packet_path, blind_saved
   189→
   190→
   191→def _do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   192→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   193→    paths = _resolve_batch_paths()
   194→    runtime_project_root = paths.project_root
   195→    batch_timeout_seconds = coerce_positive_int(
   196→        getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
   197→        default=CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   198→        minimum=1,
   199→    )
   200→    batch_max_retries = coerce_positive_int(
   201→        getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
   202→        default=1,
   203→        minimum=0,
   204→    )
   205→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = coerce_non_negative_float(
   206→        getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
   207→        default=2.0,
   208→    )
   209→    batch_heartbeat_seconds = coerce_non_negative_float(
   210→        getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
   211→        default=15.0,
   212→    )
   213→    batch_live_log_interval_seconds = (
   214→        max(1.0, min(batch_heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   215→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   216→        else 5.0
   217→    )
   218→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = coerce_non_negative_int(
   219→        getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None),
   220→        default=CODEX_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS,
   221→    )
   222→
   223→    deps = review_batches_mod.BatchRunDependencies(
   224→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=(
   225→            lambda batch_args, *, state, lang, config, stamp: _load_or_prepare_packet(
   226→                batch_args,
   227→                state=state,
   228→                lang=lang,
   229→                config=config,
   230→                stamp=stamp,
   231→                paths=paths,
   232→            )
   233→        ),
   234→        run_codex_batch_fn=(
   235→            lambda *, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file: runner_helpers_mod.run_codex_batch(
   236→                prompt=prompt,
   237→                repo_root=repo_root,
   238→                output_file=output_file,
   239→                log_file=log_file,
   240→                deps=runner_helpers_mod.CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
   241→                    timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   242→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   243→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   244→                    safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   245→                    use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
   246→                    subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
   247→                    live_log_interval_seconds=batch_live_log_interval_seconds,
   248→                    stall_after_output_seconds=batch_stall_kill_seconds,
   249→                    max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   250→                    retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   251→                ),
   252→            )
   253→        ),
   254→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   255→        do_import_fn=_do_import,
   256→        run_followup_scan_fn=(
   257→            lambda *, lang_name, scan_path: runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   258→                lang_name=lang_name,
   259→                scan_path=scan_path,
   260→                deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   261→                    project_root=runtime_project_root,
   262→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   263→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   264→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   265→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   266→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   267→                ),
   268→            )
   269→        ),
   270→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   271→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   272→        project_root=runtime_project_root,
   273→        subagent_runs_dir=paths.subagent_runs_dir,
   274→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   275→    )
   276→    return review_batches_mod.do_run_batches(
   277→        args,
   278→        state,
   279→        lang,
   280→        state_file,
   281→        config=config,
   282→        deps=deps,
   283→    )
   284→

   147→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
   148→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():

   150→    try:

   181→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:

   318→            "2. Start from the template JSON so `session.id` and `session.token` are preserved.",
   319→            f"3. Keep `session.id` exactly `{session_id}`.",
   320→            f"4. Keep `session.token` exactly `{token}`.",
   321→            "5. Output must be valid JSON with top-level keys: session, assessments, findings.",
   322→            "6. Every finding must include: dimension, identifier, summary, related_files, evidence, suggestion, confidence.",
   323→            "7. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).",
   324→            "8. Return JSON only (no markdown fences).",
   325→        ]
   326→    )
   327→
   328→
   329→def do_external_start(args, state, lang, *, config: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   330→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""
   331→    config = config or {}
   332→    paths = _resolve_external_paths()
   333→    runner = str(getattr(args, "external_runner", "claude")).strip().lower()
   334→    if runner not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   335→        print(
   336→            colorize(
   337→                f"  Error: unsupported external runner '{runner}'. Supported: claude.",
   338→                "red",
   339→            ),
   340→            file=sys.stderr,
   341→        )
   342→        sys.exit(2)
   343→    ttl_hours = int(getattr(args, "session_ttl_hours", 24) or 0)
   344→    if ttl_hours <= 0:
   345→        print(colorize("  Error: --session-ttl-hours must be > 0.", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   346→        sys.exit(2)
   347→
   348→    packet, packet_path, blind_path = _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   349→        args,
   350→        state,
   351→        lang,
   352→        config=config,
   353→        paths=paths,
   354→    )
   355→    packet_hash = runner_helpers_mod.sha256_file(blind_path)
   356→    if not isinstance(packet_hash, str):
   357→        print(
   358→            colorize(f"  Error: failed to hash blind packet: {blind_path}", "red"),
   359→            file=sys.stderr,
   360→        )
   361→        sys.exit(1)
   362→
   363→    now = _utc_now()
   364→    expires = now + timedelta(hours=ttl_hours)
   365→    session_id = _session_id()
   366→    [REDACTED](16)
   367→    session_dir = _session_dir(session_id, paths=paths)
   368→    session_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   369→
   370→    template_payload = _build_template_payload(packet, session_id=session_id, token=token)
   371→    template_path = session_dir / "review_result.template.json"
   372→    instructions_path = session_dir / "reviewer_instructions.md"
   373→    launch_prompt_path = session_dir / "claude_launch_prompt.md"
   374→    output_path = session_dir / "review_result.json"
   375→    session_path = _session_file(session_id, paths=paths)
   376→
   377→    session_payload = {
   378→        "session_id": session_id,
   379→        "status": "open",
   380→        "runner": runner,
   381→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(now),
   382→        "expires_at": _iso_seconds(expires),
   383→        "ttl_hours": ttl_hours,
   384→        "token": token,
   385→        "attest": EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT,
   386→        "packet_path": str(packet_path),

   389→        "template_path": str(template_path),
   390→        "launch_prompt_path": str(launch_prompt_path),
   391→        "instructions_path": str(instructions_path),
   392→        "expected_output_path": str(output_path),
   393→    }
   394→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   395→    safe_write_text(template_path, json.dumps(template_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   396→    safe_write_text(
   397→        launch_prompt_path,
   398→        _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   399→            session_id=session_id,
   400→            token=token,
   401→            blind_path=blind_path,
   402→            template_path=template_path,
   403→            output_path=output_path,
   404→        )
   405→        + "\n",
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    instructions = "\n".join(
   409→        [
   410→            "# External Blind Review Session",
   411→            "",
   412→            f"Session id: {session_id}",
   413→            f"Session token: {token}",
   414→            f"Blind packet: {blind_path}",
   415→            f"Template output: {template_path}",

   460→
   461→def _canonical_external_payload(
   462→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   463→    *,
   464→    session: dict[str, Any],
   465→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   466→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   467→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   468→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   469→        print(
   470→            colorize(
   471→                "  Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object.",
   472→                "red",
   473→            ),
   474→            file=sys.stderr,
   475→        )
   476→        print(
   477→            colorize(
   478→                "  Expected: {\"session\":{\"id\":\"...\",\"token\":\"...\"},\"assessments\":{...},\"findings\":[...]}",
   479→                "dim",
   480→            ),
   481→            file=sys.stderr,
   482→        )
   483→        sys.exit(1)
   484→
   485→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   486→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   487→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   488→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   489→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   490→        print(
   491→            colorize(
   492→                "  Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload.",
   493→                "red",
   494→            ),
   495→            file=sys.stderr,
   496→        )
   497→        print(
   498→            colorize("  Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.", "yellow"),
   499→            file=sys.stderr,
   500→        )
   501→        sys.exit(1)
   502→
   503→    payload = {
   504→        key: value
   505→        for key, value in raw_payload.items()
   506→        if key not in {"session", "provenance"}
   507→    }
   508→    payload["provenance"] = {
   509→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   510→        "blind": True,
   511→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   512→        "run_stamp": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   513→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   514→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   515→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   516→        "external_session_id": expected_id,
   517→    }
   518→    return payload
   519→
   520→
   521→def _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   522→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   523→    if status == "open":
   524→        return
   525→    print(
   526→        colorize(
   527→            f"  Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   528→            "red",
   529→        ),
   530→        file=sys.stderr,
   531→    )
   532→    sys.exit(1)
   533→
   534→
   535→def _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   536→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   537→    if expires_at is None:
   538→        print(
   539→            colorize(
   540→                "  Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   541→                "red",
   542→            ),
   543→            file=sys.stderr,
   544→        )
   545→        sys.exit(1)
   546→    now = _utc_now()
   547→    if now <= expires_at:
   548→        return
   549→    print(
   550→        colorize(
   551→            f"  Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   552→            "red",
   553→        ),
   554→        file=sys.stderr,
   555→    )
   556→    sys.exit(1)
   557→
   558→
   559→def do_external_submit(
   560→    *,
   561→    import_file: str,
   562→    session_id: str,
   563→    state: dict,
   564→    lang,
   565→    state_file,
   566→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   567→    options: ExternalSubmitOptions | None = None,
   568→    **legacy_options: Any,
   569→) -> None:
   570→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   571→    submit_options = _resolve_external_submit_options(
   572→        options=options,
   573→        **legacy_options,
   574→    )
   575→    config = config or {}
   576→    paths = _resolve_external_paths()
   577→    session_path, session = _session_payload_or_exit(session_id, paths=paths)
   578→    _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session)
   579→    _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session)
   580→
   581→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   582→        print(
   583→            colorize(
   584→                "  Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   585→                "red",
   586→            ),
   587→            file=sys.stderr,
   588→        )
   589→        sys.exit(1)
   590→
   591→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   592→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(findings_path, label="external findings")
   593→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   594→
   595→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
   596→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   597→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   598→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   599→
   600→    if submit_options.dry_run:
   601→        do_validate_import(
   602→            str(canonical_path),
   603→            lang,
   604→            allow_partial=submit_options.allow_partial,
   605→            attested_external=True,
   606→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   607→        )
   608→        return
   609→
   610→    do_import(
   611→        str(canonical_path),
   612→        state,
   613→        lang,
   614→        state_file,
   615→        config=config,
   616→        allow_partial=submit_options.allow_partial,
   617→        attested_external=True,
   618→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   619→        force_review_import=submit_options.force_review_import,
   620→    )
   621→
   622→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   623→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   624→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   625→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(findings_path)
   626→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   627→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   628→
   629→    if submit_options.scan_after_import:
   630→        code = runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   631→            lang_name=lang.name,
   632→            scan_path=submit_options.scan_path,
   633→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   634→                project_root=paths.project_root,
   635→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   636→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   637→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   638→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   639→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   640→            ),
   641→        )
   642→        if code != 0:
   643→            raise SystemExit(code)
   644→
   645→
   646→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   647→

    12→def _metadata_helpers():
    13→    metadata_mod = importlib.import_module(
    14→        "desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata"
    15→    )
    16→    return (
    17→        metadata_mod.dimension_display_name,
    18→        metadata_mod.dimension_weight,

    29→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    30→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    31→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    32→
    33→
    34→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    35→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    36→        return ""
    37→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    38→
    39→
    40→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    41→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    42→    for finding in findings.values():
    43→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    44→            continue
    45→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    46→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    47→            continue
    48→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    49→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    50→    if not counts:
    51→        return None
    52→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    53→
    54→
    55→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    56→    try:
    57→        dimension_display_name, _ = _metadata_helpers()
    58→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    59→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    60→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    64→    try:
    65→        _, dimension_weight = _metadata_helpers()
    66→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    67→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    68→        return 1.0
    69→
    70→
    71→def _normalize_default_dimensions(
    72→    raw_defaults: list[str],
    73→    *,
    74→    allowed: set[str] | None,
    75→) -> list[str]:
    76→    default_dimensions: list[str] = []
    77→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
    78→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
    79→        if not dim:
    80→            continue
    81→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
    82→            continue
    83→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
    84→    return default_dimensions
    85→
    86→
    87→def _normalize_assessed_dimensions(
    88→    assessments: dict | None,
    89→    *,
    90→    allowed: set[str] | None,
    91→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    92→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
    93→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
    94→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
    95→        if not dim:
    96→            continue
    97→        # Placeholder assessments are scanner-generated and should follow
    98→        # allowed-dimension scope. Real imports are always retained.
    99→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   100→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   101→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   102→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   103→        )
   104→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   105→            continue
   106→        assessed[dim] = payload
   107→    return assessed

   110→def _issue_count_for_dimension(
   111→    findings: dict,
   112→    *,
   113→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
   114→    dim_name: str,
   115→) -> int:
   116→    return sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for finding in findings.values()
   119→        if finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   120→        and finding.get("status") in failure_set
   121→        and _normalize_dimension_key(
   122→            finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   123→        )
   124→        == dim_name
   125→    )

   128→def _score_from_assessment(assessment: dict | None) -> tuple[float, float, float, bool, bool]:
   129→    has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   130→    assessment_score = (
   131→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
   132→        if has_assessment
   133→        else 0.0
   134→    )
   135→    integrity_penalty = (
   136→        assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
   137→        if has_assessment
   138→        else None
   139→    )
   140→    reset_pending = bool(
   141→        has_assessment
   142→        and (
   143→            assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   144→            or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   145→            or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→    if reset_pending or integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
   149→        score = 0.0
   150→        pass_rate = 0.0
   151→    elif has_assessment:
   152→        score = assessment_score
   153→        pass_rate = score / 100.0
   154→    else:
   155→        score = 0.0
   156→        pass_rate = 0.0
   157→    return score, pass_rate, assessment_score, reset_pending, has_assessment
   158→
   159→
   160→def _component_payload(assessment: dict | None) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, float]]:
   161→    components: list[str] = []
   162→    component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   163→    if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
   164→        return components, component_scores
   165→
   166→    raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   167→    if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   168→        components = [
   169→            str(item).strip()
   170→            for item in raw_components
   171→            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   172→        ]
   173→
   174→    raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   175→    if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   176→        for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   177→            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   178→                continue
   179→            if not is_numeric(value):
   180→                continue
   181→            component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   182→                max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   183→                1,
   184→            )
   185→    return components, component_scores
   186→
   187→
   188→def _build_subjective_detector_payload(
   189→    *,
   190→    pass_rate: float,
   191→    issue_count: int,
   192→    assessment_score: float,
   193→    reset_pending: bool,
   194→    has_assessment: bool,
   195→    dim_name: str,
   196→    lang_name: str | None,
   197→    components: list[str],
   198→    component_scores: dict[str, float],
   199→) -> dict:
   200→    payload = {
   201→        "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   202→        "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   203→        "issues": issue_count,
   204→        "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   205→        "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   206→        "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   207→        "dimension_key": dim_name,
   208→        "configured_weight": round(
   209→            _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   210→        ),
   211→        "components": components,
   212→    }
   213→    if component_scores:
   214→        payload["component_scores"] = component_scores
   215→    return payload
   216→

   218→def append_subjective_dimensions(
   219→    results: dict,
   220→    findings: dict,
   221→    assessments: dict | None,
   222→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
   223→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
   226→
   227→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review findings for a dimension
   228→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
   229→    metadata for transparency.
   230→    """
   231→    raw_defaults = _holistic_dimensions()
   232→    allowed = (
   233→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   234→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   235→        else None
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    default_dimensions = _normalize_default_dimensions(raw_defaults, allowed=allowed)
   239→    assessed = _normalize_assessed_dimensions(assessments, allowed=allowed)
   240→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   241→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_findings(findings)
   242→
   243→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   244→    for dim_name in assessed:
   245→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   246→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   247→
   248→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   249→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   250→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   251→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   252→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   253→            continue
   254→
   255→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   256→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   257→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   258→
   259→        issue_count = _issue_count_for_dimension(
   260→            findings,
   261→            failure_set=failure_set,
   262→            dim_name=dim_name,
   263→        )
   264→        score, pass_rate, assessment_score, reset_pending, has_assessment = (
   265→            _score_from_assessment(assessment if isinstance(assessment, dict) else None)
   266→        )
   267→        components, component_scores = _component_payload(
   268→            assessment if isinstance(assessment, dict) else None
   269→        )
   270→
   271→        results[display] = {
   272→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   273→            "tier": 4,
   274→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   275→            "issues": issue_count,
   276→            "detectors": {
   277→                "subjective_assessment": _build_subjective_detector_payload(
   278→                    pass_rate=pass_rate,
   279→                    issue_count=issue_count,
   280→                    assessment_score=assessment_score,
   281→                    reset_pending=reset_pending,
   282→                    has_assessment=has_assessment,
   283→                    dim_name=dim_name,
   284→                    lang_name=lang_name,
   285→                    components=components,
   286→                    component_scores=component_scores,
   287→                )
   288→            },
   289→        }
   290→
   291→
   292→__all__ = [
   293→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   294→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   295→]
   296→

1→"""Shared review packet preparation helpers used by prepare/batch flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence import review as review_mod
     9→
    10→from .constants import DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES
    11→
    12→
    13→def redacted_review_config(config: dict[str, object] | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    14→    """Return review config payload with score-target hints removed."""
    15→    if not isinstance(config, dict):
    16→        return {}
    17→    return {key: value for key, value in config.items() if key != "target_strict_score"}
    18→
    19→
    20→def coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config: dict[str, object] | None) -> int | None:
    21→    """Resolve per-batch review file cap from config (0/negative => unlimited)."""
    22→    raw = (config or {}).get("review_batch_max_files", DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES)
    23→    try:
    24→        value = int(raw)
    25→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    26→        return DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES
    27→    return value if value > 0 else None
    28→
    29→
    30→def build_run_batches_next_command(
    31→    *,
    32→    include_scan_after_import: bool,
    33→    retrospective: bool,
    34→    retrospective_max_issues: int,
    35→    retrospective_max_batch_items: int,
    36→) -> str:
    37→    """Build canonical follow-up command for batch execution."""
    38→    command = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel"
    39→    if include_scan_after_import:
    40→        command += " --scan-after-import"
    41→    if retrospective:
    42→        command += (
    43→            " --retrospective"
    44→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
    45→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
    46→        )
    47→    return command
    48→
    49→
    50→def build_review_packet(
    51→    *,
    52→    path: Path,
    53→    lang_run,
    54→    state: dict[str, Any],
    55→    config: dict[str, object] | None,
    56→    dimensions: list[str] | None,
    57→    files: list[str] | None,
    58→    narrative: dict[str, Any],
    59→    retrospective: bool,
    60→    retrospective_max_issues: int,
    61→    retrospective_max_batch_items: int,
    62→    include_scan_after_import: bool,
    63→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    64→    """Create the canonical holistic review packet payload."""
    65→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
    66→        path,
    67→        lang_run,
    68→        state,
    69→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    70→            dimensions=dimensions,
    71→            files=files or None,
    72→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    73→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
    74→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    75→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    76→        ),
    77→    )
    78→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
    79→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
    80→    packet["next_command"] = build_run_batches_next_command(
    81→        include_scan_after_import=include_scan_after_import,
    82→        retrospective=retrospective,
    83→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    84→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    85→    )
    86→    return packet
    87→
    88→
    89→__all__ = [
    90→    "build_review_packet",
    91→    "build_run_batches_next_command",
    92→    "coerce_review_batch_file_limit",
    93→    "redacted_review_config",
    94→]
    95→

    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class _PromptBatchContext:
    18→    name: str
    19→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...]
    20→    rationale: str
    21→    seed_files: tuple[str, ...]
    22→    findings_cap: int
    23→
    24→    @property
    25→    def dimension_set(self) -> set[str]:
    26→        return set(self.dimensions)
    27→
    28→    @property
    29→    def dimensions_text(self) -> str:
    30→        return ", ".join(self.dimensions) if self.dimensions else "(none)"
    31→
    32→    @property

    34→        return "\n".join(f"- {path}" for path in self.seed_files) if self.seed_files else "- (none)"

    36→
    37→def _coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    38→    if not isinstance(raw, list | tuple):
    39→        return ()
    40→    return tuple(str(item) for item in raw if isinstance(item, str) and item)
    41→
    42→
    43→def _build_context(batch: dict[str, object], batch_index: int) -> _PromptBatchContext:
    44→    dimensions = _coerce_string_list(batch.get("dimensions", []))

    46→        name=str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}")),
    47→        dimensions=dimensions,

    99→def _render_historical_focus(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   100→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
   101→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
   102→        return ""
   103→
   104→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   105→    try:
   106→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   107→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   108→        selected_count = 0
   109→
   110→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   111→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   112→        issues = []
   113→
   114→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   115→        return ""
   116→
   117→    lines: list[str] = []
   118→    lines.append(
   119→        "Previously flagged issues (from past reviews of these dimensions):"
   120→    )
   121→    lines.append(
   122→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
   123→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
   124→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    for entry in issues:
   128→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   129→            continue
   130→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   131→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   132→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   133→
   134→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
   135→        if note:
   136→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   137→        lines.append(line)
   138→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   139→
   140→
   141→def _render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   142→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   143→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   144→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   145→        return ""
   146→
   147→    lines: list[str] = []
   148→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals (detector synthesis hypotheses):")
   149→    lines.append(
   150→        "Treat each as a hypothesis: confirm or refute with direct code evidence."
   151→    )
   152→
   153→    rendered = 0
   154→    for entry in signals:
   155→        rendered_lines = _render_mechanical_signal_entry(entry)
   156→        if not rendered_lines:
   157→            continue
   158→        lines.extend(rendered_lines)
   159→        rendered += 1
   160→    if rendered < len(signals):
   161→        lines.append(
   162→            f"  - (skipped {len(signals) - rendered} malformed concern signal entries)"
   163→        )
   164→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   165→
   166→
   167→def _coerce_signal_evidence(entry: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:
   168→    evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   169→    if not isinstance(evidence_raw, list):
   170→        return []
   171→    return [
   172→        str(item).strip()
   173→        for item in evidence_raw
   174→        if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   175→    ]
   176→
   177→
   178→def _render_mechanical_signal_entry(entry: object) -> list[str]:
   179→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   180→        return []
   181→    file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   182→    concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   183→    summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   184→    question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   185→    evidence = _coerce_signal_evidence(entry)
   186→
   187→    lines = [f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}"]
   188→    if summary:
   189→        lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   190→    if question:
   191→        lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   192→    for snippet in evidence[:2]:
   193→        lines.append(f"    evidence: {snippet}")
   194→    return lines
   195→
   196→
   197→def _render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   198→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   199→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   200→        {
   201→            "cross_module_architecture",
   202→            "high_level_elegance",
   203→            "mid_level_elegance",
   204→            "design_coherence",
   205→            "initialization_coupling",
   206→        }
   207→    ):
   208→        return ""
   209→    return (
   210→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   211→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   212→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   213→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   214→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   215→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   216→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   217→    )
   218→
   219→
   220→def _render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   221→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   222→        return ""
   223→    return (
   224→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   225→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   226→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   227→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   228→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   229→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   230→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_findings` for directories "
   231→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   232→    )
   233→
   234→
   235→def _render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   236→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   237→        return ""
   238→    return (
   239→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   240→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   241→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   242→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   243→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   244→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   245→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   246→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   247→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   248→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   249→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   250→    )

   252→
   253→def _render_dimension_focus(context: _PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   254→    dim_set = context.dimension_set
   255→    return (
   256→        _render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   257→        + _render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   258→        + _render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   259→        + _render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   260→    )

   295→def _render_task_requirements(context: _PromptBatchContext) -> str:
   296→    return (
   297→        "Task requirements:\n"
   298→        "1. Read the blind packet and follow `system_prompt` constraints exactly.\n"
   299→        "1a. If previously flagged issues are listed above, use them as context for your review.\n"
   300→        "    Verify whether each still applies to the current code. Do not re-report fixed or\n"
   301→        "    wontfix issues. Use them as starting points to look deeper — inspect adjacent code\n"
   302→        "    and related modules for defects the prior review may have missed.\n"
   303→        "1b. If mechanical concern signals are listed above, explicitly confirm or refute them.\n"
   304→        "    Report confirmed defects under the most impacted batch dimension.\n"
   305→        "    If refuting, include clear counter-evidence in `dimension_notes`.\n"
   306→        "1c. Think structurally: when you spot multiple individual issues that share a common\n"
   307→        "    root cause (missing abstraction, duplicated pattern, inconsistent convention),\n"
   308→        "    explain the deeper structural issue in the finding, not just the surface symptom.\n"
   309→        "    If the pattern is significant enough, report the structural issue as its own finding\n"
   310→        "    with appropriate fix_scope ('multi_file_refactor' or 'architectural_change') and\n"
   311→        "    use `root_cause_cluster` to connect related symptom findings together.\n"
   312→        "2. Start with the seed files, then freely explore additional repository files likely to surface material issues.\n"
   313→        "2a. Prioritize high-signal leads: unexplored/lightly reviewed files, historical issue areas, and hotspot neighbors "

   316→        "2c. Keep findings and scoring scoped to this batch's listed dimensions.\n"
   317→        "2d. Respect scope controls in the blind packet config: do not include files/directories marked by "
   318→        "`exclude`, `ignore`, or zone overrides that classify files as non-production (test/config/generated/vendor).\n"
   319→        f"3. Return 0-{context.findings_cap} high-quality findings for this batch (empty array allowed).\n"
   320→        "3a. Do not suppress real defects to keep scores high; report every material issue you can support with evidence.\n"
   321→        "3b. Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary evidence in this batch.\n"
   322→        "4. Score/finding consistency is required: broader or more severe findings MUST lower dimension scores.\n"
   323→        f"4a. Any dimension scored below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit feedback: add at least one "
   324→        "finding with the same `dimension` and a non-empty actionable `suggestion`.\n"
   325→        "5. Every finding must include `related_files` with at least 2 files when possible.\n"
   326→        "6. Every finding must include `dimension`, `identifier`, `summary`, `evidence`, `suggestion`, and `confidence`.\n"
   327→        "7. Every finding must include `impact_scope` and `fix_scope`.\n"
   328→        "8. Every scored dimension MUST include dimension_notes with concrete evidence.\n"
   329→        f"9. If a dimension score is >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}, include `{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}` in dimension_notes.\n"
   330→        "10. Use exactly one decimal place for every assessment and abstraction sub-axis score.\n"
   331→        f"{_render_dimension_focus(context)}"
   332→        "11. Ignore prior chat context and any target-threshold assumptions.\n"
   333→        "12. Do not edit repository files.\n"
   334→        "13. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.\n\n"
   335→    )
   336→
   337→
   338→def _render_scope_enums() -> str:
   339→    return (
   340→        "Scope enums:\n"
   341→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   342→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   343→    )
   344→
   345→
   346→def _render_output_schema(context: _PromptBatchContext, batch_index: int) -> str:
   347→    return (
   348→        "Output schema:\n"
   349→        "{\n"
   350→        f'  "batch": "{context.name}",\n'
   351→        f'  "batch_index": {batch_index + 1},\n'
   352→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'
   353→        '  "dimension_notes": {\n'
   354→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
   355→        '      "evidence": ["specific code observations"],\n'
   356→        '      "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   357→        '      "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   358→        '      "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
   359→        f'      "{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}": "required when score >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}",\n'
   360→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
   361→        "    }\n"
   362→        "  },\n"
   363→        '  "findings": [{\n'
   364→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
   365→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
   366→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
   367→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
   368→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
   369→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
   370→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
   371→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   372→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   373→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history"\n'
   374→        "  }],\n"
   375→        '  "retrospective": {\n'
   376→        '    "root_causes": ["optional: concise root-cause hypotheses"],\n'
   377→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
   378→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
   379→        "  }\n"
   380→        "}\n"
   381→    )
   382→
   383→
   384→def _join_non_empty_sections(*sections: str) -> str:
   385→    return "".join(section for section in sections if section)
   386→
   387→
   388→def render_batch_prompt(
   389→    *,
   390→    repo_root: Path,
   391→    packet_path: Path,
   392→    batch_index: int,
   393→    batch: dict[str, object],
   394→) -> str:
   395→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   396→    context = _build_context(batch, batch_index)
   397→    return _join_non_empty_sections(
   398→        _render_metadata_block(
   399→            repo_root=repo_root,
   400→            packet_path=packet_path,
   401→            batch_index=batch_index,
   402→            context=context,
   403→        ),
   404→        _render_scan_evidence_note(),
   405→        _render_seed_files_block(context),
   406→        _render_historical_focus(batch),
   407→        _render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   408→        _render_task_requirements(context),
   409→        _render_scope_enums(),
   410→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   411→    )
   412→
   413→
   414→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
   415→

1→"""Scoring primitives for holistic review batch merges."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→_CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {
     9→    "high": 1.2,
    10→    "medium": 1.0,
    11→    "low": 0.75,
    12→}
    13→_IMPACT_SCOPE_WEIGHTS = {
    14→    "local": 1.0,
    15→    "module": 1.3,
    16→    "subsystem": 1.6,
    17→    "codebase": 2.0,
    18→}
    19→_FIX_SCOPE_WEIGHTS = {
    20→    "single_edit": 1.0,
    21→    "multi_file_refactor": 1.3,
    22→    "architectural_change": 1.7,
    23→}
    24→
    25→# Blending ratio between weighted mean and per-batch floor score.
    26→_WEIGHTED_MEAN_BLEND = 0.7
    27→_FLOOR_BLEND_WEIGHT = 0.3
    28→
    29→# Maximum total penalty that findings can impose on a dimension score.
    30→_MAX_ISSUE_PENALTY = 24.0
    31→
    32→# Per-unit penalty from cumulative finding severity.
    33→_PRESSURE_PENALTY_MULTIPLIER = 2.2
    34→
    35→# Extra penalty per additional finding beyond the first.
    36→_EXTRA_FINDING_PENALTY = 0.8
    37→
    38→# Findings-based score cap parameters.
    39→_CAP_FLOOR = 60.0
    40→_CAP_CEILING = 90.0
    41→_CAP_PRESSURE_MULTIPLIER = 3.5
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class ScoreInputs:
    46→    """Normalized inputs for a single dimension merge computation."""
    47→
    48→    weighted_mean: float
    49→    floor: float
    50→    finding_pressure: float
    51→    finding_count: int
    52→
    53→
    54→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    55→class ScoreBreakdown:
    56→    """Named intermediate values for one merged dimension score."""
    57→
    58→    weighted_mean: float
    59→    floor: float
    60→    floor_aware: float
    61→    issue_penalty: float
    62→    issue_cap: float | None
    63→    final_score: float
    64→
    65→
    66→class DimensionMergeScorer:
    67→    """Compute pressure-adjusted merged scores for holistic review dimensions."""
    68→
    69→    def finding_severity(
    70→        self,
    71→        finding: dict[str, Any],
    72→        *,
    73→        note: dict[str, Any] | None,
    74→    ) -> float:
    75→        """Compute per-finding severity used for score-pressure adjustments."""
    76→        note_ref = note if isinstance(note, dict) else {}
    77→        confidence = str(
    78→            finding.get("confidence", note_ref.get("confidence", "medium"))
    79→        ).strip().lower()
    80→        impact_scope = str(
    81→            finding.get("impact_scope", note_ref.get("impact_scope", "local"))
    82→        ).strip().lower()
    83→        fix_scope = str(
    84→            finding.get("fix_scope", note_ref.get("fix_scope", "single_edit"))
    85→        ).strip().lower()
    86→
    87→        confidence_weight = _CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(confidence, 1.0)
    88→        impact_weight = _IMPACT_SCOPE_WEIGHTS.get(impact_scope, 1.0)
    89→        fix_weight = _FIX_SCOPE_WEIGHTS.get(fix_scope, 1.0)
    90→        return confidence_weight * impact_weight * fix_weight
    91→
    92→    def finding_pressure_by_dimension(
    93→        self,
    94→        findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
    95→        *,
    96→        dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    97→    ) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, int]]:
    98→        """Summarize how strongly findings should pull dimension scores down."""
    99→        pressure_by_dim: dict[str, float] = {}
   100→        count_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   101→        for finding in findings:
   102→            dim = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   103→            if not dim:
   104→                continue
   105→            note = dimension_notes.get(dim)
   106→            pressure_by_dim[dim] = pressure_by_dim.get(dim, 0.0) + self.finding_severity(
   107→                finding,
   108→                note=note if isinstance(note, dict) else None,
   109→            )
   110→            count_by_dim[dim] = count_by_dim.get(dim, 0) + 1
   111→        return pressure_by_dim, count_by_dim
   112→
   113→    def score_dimension(self, inputs: ScoreInputs) -> ScoreBreakdown:
   114→        """Compute one merged score with explicit intermediate values."""
   115→        floor_aware = (
   116→            _WEIGHTED_MEAN_BLEND * inputs.weighted_mean
   117→            + _FLOOR_BLEND_WEIGHT * inputs.floor
   118→        )
   119→        issue_penalty = min(
   120→            _MAX_ISSUE_PENALTY,
   121→            (inputs.finding_pressure * _PRESSURE_PENALTY_MULTIPLIER)
   122→            + (max(inputs.finding_count - 1, 0) * _EXTRA_FINDING_PENALTY),
   123→        )
   124→        issue_adjusted = floor_aware - issue_penalty
   125→
   126→        issue_cap: float | None = None
   127→        if inputs.finding_count > 0:
   128→            cap_penalty = (
   129→                (inputs.finding_pressure * _CAP_PRESSURE_MULTIPLIER)
   130→                + (max(inputs.finding_count - 1, 0) * _EXTRA_FINDING_PENALTY)
   131→            )
   132→            issue_cap = max(
   133→                _CAP_FLOOR,
   134→                _CAP_CEILING - cap_penalty,
   135→            )
   136→            issue_adjusted = min(issue_adjusted, issue_cap)
   137→
   138→        final_score = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, issue_adjusted)), 1)
   139→        return ScoreBreakdown(
   140→            weighted_mean=inputs.weighted_mean,
   141→            floor=inputs.floor,
   142→            floor_aware=floor_aware,
   143→            issue_penalty=issue_penalty,
   144→            issue_cap=issue_cap,
   145→            final_score=final_score,
   146→        )
   147→
   148→    def merge_scores(
   149→        self,
   150→        score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   151→        score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   152→        finding_pressure_by_dim: dict[str, float],
   153→        finding_count_by_dim: dict[str, int],
   154→    ) -> dict[str, float]:
   155→        """Compute pressure-adjusted weighted mean for each dimension."""
   156→        merged: dict[str, float] = {}
   157→        for key, weighted_scores in sorted(score_buckets.items()):
   158→            if not weighted_scores:
   159→                continue
   160→            numerator = sum(score * weight for score, weight in weighted_scores)
   161→            denominator = sum(weight for _, weight in weighted_scores)
   162→            weighted_mean = numerator / max(denominator, 1.0)
   163→            floor = min(score_raw_by_dim.get(key, [weighted_mean]))
   164→            breakdown = self.score_dimension(
   165→                ScoreInputs(
   166→                    weighted_mean=weighted_mean,
   167→                    floor=floor,
   168→                    finding_pressure=finding_pressure_by_dim.get(key, 0.0),
   169→                    finding_count=finding_count_by_dim.get(key, 0),
   170→                )
   171→            )
   172→            merged[key] = breakdown.final_score
   173→        return merged
   174→
   175→
   176→__all__ = ["DimensionMergeScorer", "ScoreBreakdown", "ScoreInputs"]
   177→

1→"""Shared subjective-review contract for prompts, normalization, and import validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     6→ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD = 100.0
     7→HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     8→[REDACTED]

    10→[REDACTED]
    11→[REDACTED]
    12→DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_FINDINGS = 10

    14→
    15→# Backward compatibility alias for older imports.

    17→
    18→
    19→def max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(dimension_count: int) -> int:
    20→    """Return the normalized max findings budget for one batch payload."""
    21→    safe_count = max(0, int(dimension_count))
    22→    return max(DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_FINDINGS, safe_count)
    23→
    24→
    25→def score_requires_dimension_finding(score: float) -> bool:
    26→    """Return True when score requires at least one explicit finding."""
    27→    return float(score) < LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD
    28→
    29→
    30→def score_requires_explicit_feedback(score: float) -> bool:
    31→    """Return True when score requires a finding or dimension-note evidence."""
    32→    return float(score) < ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD
    33→
    34→
    35→def global_prompt_contract() -> str:
    36→    """Render shared review contract text injected into all review prompts."""
    37→    return (
    38→        f"{_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER}\n"
    39→        "- Scope breadth: report any material issues supported by evidence "
    40→        "(structural, architectural, boundary, readability, lifecycle), "
    41→        "not only low-level nits.\n"
    42→        "- Dimension boundaries are guidance, not a gag-order: if an issue "
    43→        "spans dimensions, report it under the most impacted dimension.\n"
    44→        "- Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary code "
    45→        "with clear positive evidence; if there is uncertainty or residual "
    46→        "issues, score below 100.\n"
    47→        "- Do not suppress valid findings to keep scores high.\n"
    48→        f"- Scores below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include at least one "
    49→        "finding for that same dimension.\n"
    50→        f"- Scores below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit "
    51→        "feedback for that same dimension (finding with suggestion or "
    52→        "dimension_notes evidence).\n"
    53→        f"- Scores above {HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include a "
    54→        "non-empty `issues_preventing_higher_score` note for that dimension.\n"
    55→        "- Findings must always describe defects that need change, never positive observations.\n"
    56→        "- Think structurally: when individual findings form a pattern, consider what is\n"

    58→        "  repeated pattern, inconsistent convention), say so in the findings — explain the\n"

    60→    )

    63→def ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt: str) -> str:

   751→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   752→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
   753→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
   754→
   755→
   756→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   757→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
   758→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
   759→    for key, value in config.items():
   760→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
   761→        if not lowered:
   762→            continue
   763→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
   764→            continue
   765→        if "target" in lowered:
   766→            continue
   767→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
   768→            continue
   769→        sanitized[key] = value
   770→    return sanitized
   771→
   772→
   773→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   774→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
   775→    try:
   776→        data = path.read_bytes()
   777→    except OSError:
   778→        return None
   779→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   780→
   781→
   782→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   783→    *,
   784→    runner: str,
   785→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   786→    run_stamp: str,
   787→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   788→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   789→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   790→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   791→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   792→    return {
   793→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   794→        "blind": True,
   795→        "runner": runner,
   796→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   797→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   798→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   799→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   800→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   801→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   802→    }
   803→
   804→
   805→def selected_batch_indexes(
   806→    *,
   807→    raw_selection: str | None,
   808→    batch_count: int,
   809→    parse_fn,
   810→    colorize_fn,
   811→) -> list[int]:
   812→    """Validate selected batch indexes or exit with a CLI error."""
   813→    try:
   814→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   815→    except ValueError as exc:
   816→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   817→        sys.exit(2)
   818→    if selected:
   819→        return selected
   820→    print(colorize_fn("  Error: no batches selected", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   821→    sys.exit(2)
   822→
   823→
   824→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   825→    *,
   826→    stamp: str,
   827→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   828→    batches: list[dict],
   829→    packet_path: Path,

1→"""File selection and context section builders for holistic review."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.pathing.api import resolve_path
     8→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.review.context import importer_count
    10→
    11→from .selection_dependencies import _dependencies_context, _in_allowed_files
    12→from .selection_sections import (
    13→    _architecture_context,
    14→    _coupling_context,
    15→    _error_strategy_context,
    16→    _naming_conventions_context,
    17→    _sibling_behavior_context,
    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→def select_holistic_files(path: Path, lang: object, files: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    22→    selected = files if files is not None else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
    23→    if not selected:
    24→        return []
    25→
    26→    zone_map = getattr(lang, "zone_map", None)
    27→    if zone_map is None:
    28→        return selected
    29→
    30→    filtered: list[str] = []
    31→    for filepath in selected:
    32→        try:
    33→            zone = zone_map.get(filepath)
    34→            zone_value = getattr(zone, "value", str(zone))
    35→        except (AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
    36→            zone_value = "production"
    37→        if zone_value in EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES:
    38→            continue
    39→        filtered.append(filepath)
    40→    return filtered
    41→
    42→
    43→def _testing_context(
    44→    lang,
    45→    state: dict,
    46→    file_contents: dict[str, str],
    47→    *,
    48→    allowed_files: set[str] | None = None,
    49→) -> dict:
    50→    testing: dict = {"total_files": len(file_contents)}
    51→    if not lang.dep_graph:
    52→        return testing
    53→
    54→    tc_findings = {
    55→        finding["file"]
    56→        for finding in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    57→        if finding.get("detector") == "test_coverage"
    58→        and finding.get("status") == "open"
    59→        and _in_allowed_files(finding.get("file", ""), allowed_files)
    60→    }
    61→    if not tc_findings:
    62→        return testing
    63→
    64→    critical_untested = []
    65→    for filepath in tc_findings:
    66→        entry = lang.dep_graph.get(resolve_path(filepath), {})
    67→        entry_importer_count = importer_count(entry)
    68→        if entry_importer_count >= 3:
    69→            critical_untested.append(
    70→                {"file": filepath, "importers": entry_importer_count}
    71→            )
    72→    testing["critical_untested"] = sorted(
    73→        critical_untested,
    74→        key=lambda item: -item["importers"],
    75→    )[:10]
    76→    return testing
    77→
    78→
    79→def _api_surface_context(lang, file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict:
    80→    api_surface_fn = getattr(lang, "review_api_surface_fn", None)
    81→    if not callable(api_surface_fn):
    82→        return {}
    83→    computed = api_surface_fn(file_contents)
    84→    return computed if isinstance(computed, dict) else {}
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "_api_surface_context",
    89→    "_architecture_context",
    90→    "_coupling_context",
    91→    "_dependencies_context",
    92→    "_error_strategy_context",
    93→    "_naming_conventions_context",
    94→    "_sibling_behavior_context",
    95→    "_testing_context",
    96→    "select_holistic_files",
    97→]
    98→
